#!/usr/bin/env python
"""拆除双曲几何后的 CPU 自检：`CAACPHQKVLattice` 必须**无几何、无半径分解、能跑通、有梯度**。

2026-10-20：用户判定双曲 HQKV 无用（四库 ΔF1 均值 −0.00085），下令拆除几何与半径分解，
只保留跨层 SAT 式 QKV 注入。这个脚本就是那次拆除的**回归门**：
它不训练、不碰 GPU、不需要数据集，`python models/test_hqkv_lattice_cpu.py` 直接跑。

## 检查项（27 条；服务器实测 27/27 PASS，其中 C6 需要 CUDA scan 算子）

A. **拆除完整性**（这是本轮的主诉求，放在最前面）
  A1 `caacp_hqkv_lattice` 模块及其依赖链**不 import** `hyperbolic`
  A2 仓库里已没有 `caacp_hqkv_v2` / `hyperbolic` 的 live import
  A3 `hyperbolic.py` 仍在（作为 v1 的历史依赖），但 lattice 模块**不引用**它
  A4 `param_report()["manifold"] == "none"`
  A5 `param_report()["radius_decomposition"] == "none"`
  A6 `param_report()["score_weighted_pooling"] == "none"`
  A7 `param_report()["geometry"] == "none"`
  A8 模块参数里**没有任何** r0 / a / radius 命名
  A9 `score_maps()` 恒为空（不再产生变化分数）

B. **反自锁不回退**（拆几何不能把这条工程资产一起丢掉）
  B1 每支 γ 初值 = 0.5（非零）
  B2 每支 γ 在 `backward()` 后梯度**全部非零**
  B3 `contrib_norm > 0`
  B4 负对照 γ=0 ⇒ `contrib_norm == 0` **且** γ 梯度全为 0（复现自锁，证明门确实在前向里）

C. **结构正确性**
  C1 Q token 数 = N（全分辨率）
  C2 K/V token 数 = N/w²（严格压缩，且无 TopK/聚类）
  C3 窗口不能整除时**报错**（拒绝静默 padding）
  C4 输出形状 = `(B, lite_dim, H0, W0)`
  C5 `rank` 不能整除 `dim` 时报错（depthwise V 的 groups 前提）
  C6 注入后 `feats[0]` 尺度一致（净网络级）

D. **参数量**
  D1 `param_report()["total_params"]` 与真实 parameter 数一致
  D2 总参数 < 0.40 M（拆除前 v2 三支为 ≈0.62 M；只保留单支应显著更小）
  D3 参数量在 `rank=32` 下的**结构性上界**内（逐 stage 手算）

E. **fail-closed 契约**
  E1 网络 `geometry="hyperbolic"` ⇒ `SystemExit`
  E2 网络 `geometry="euclidean"` ⇒ `SystemExit`
  E3 网络 `geometry="lattice"` ⇒ 正常构造
  E4 网络 `hqkv=1, hgsl=1` ⇒ `SystemExit`（几何拆了，HGSL 无输入口）
"""
import io
import os
import sys
from contextlib import redirect_stdout

import torch

_HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _HERE not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _HERE)

from model.layers.caacp_hqkv_lattice import (  # noqa: E402
    CAACPHQKVLattice,
    GAMMA_INIT,
    HQKV_FORM,
    LatticeSATAttention,
    build_caacp_hqkv_lattice,
    lattice_pool,
    rms_normalize,
)

DIMS = (96, 192, 384, 768)
HW = {0: (64, 64), 1: (32, 32), 2: (16, 16), 3: (8, 8)}
B = 2

_fail = []
_skip = []
_total = []


def check(name, cond, detail=""):
    _total.append(name)
    ok = bool(cond)
    print(f"  [{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}] {name}" + (f"  {detail}" if detail else ""))
    if not ok:
        _fail.append(name)
    return ok


def skip(name, why):
    _total.append(name)
    _skip.append(name)
    print(f"  [SKIP] {name}  {why}")
    return True


def make_feats(seed=3):
    g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
    fa, fb = [], []
    for i, d in enumerate(DIMS):
        h, w = HW[i]
        fa.append(torch.randn(B, d, h, w, generator=g))
        fb.append(torch.randn(B, d, h, w, generator=g))
    return fa, fb


def build(**kw):
    kw.setdefault("stage_dims", DIMS)
    kw.setdefault("rank", 32)
    kw.setdefault("window", 2)
    kw.setdefault("lite_dim", 192)
    kwargs = dict(stage_dims=kw.pop("stage_dims"), rank=kw.pop("rank"),
                  window=kw.pop("window"), lite_dim=kw.pop("lite_dim"))
    kwargs.update(kw)
    return CAACPHQKVLattice(**kwargs)


def main():
    print("=== 拆除回归门：CAACP-HQKV-Lattice（CPU）===")

    # ---------------- A. 拆除完整性 ----------------
    print("\n[A] 拆除完整性")
    src_file = os.path.join(_HERE, "model", "layers", "caacp_hqkv_lattice.py")
    with open(src_file, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        src = f.read()
    import_lines = [ln for ln in src.splitlines()
                    if ln.strip().startswith(("import ", "from "))]
    check("A1 lattice 的 import 里没有 hyperbolic",
          not any("hyperbolic" in ln for ln in import_lines),
          f"imports={len(import_lines)}")

    live_hits = []
    for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(_HERE):
        for fn in files:
            if not fn.endswith(".py") or fn == os.path.basename(__file__):
                continue
            p = os.path.join(root, fn)
            if "_SUPERSEDED" in p:
                continue
            try:
                with open(p, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                    txt = fh.read()
            except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
                continue
            for ln in txt.splitlines():
                s = ln.strip()
                if s.startswith(("import ", "from ")) and "caacp_hqkv_v2" in s:
                    live_hits.append(f"{fn}: {s}")
    check("A2 没有 live import 指向已删除的 caacp_hqkv_v2", not live_hits, str(live_hits[:3]))

    check("A3 hyperbolic.py 仍在（v1 历史依赖）但不在 lattice 依赖链里",
          os.path.isfile(os.path.join(_HERE, "model", "layers", "hyperbolic.py")),
          "hyperbolic.py 保留给 casa_vmamba_str v1")

    m = build()
    rep = m.param_report()
    check("A4 manifold == none", rep["manifold"] == "none", rep["manifold"])
    check("A5 radius_decomposition == none", rep["radius_decomposition"] == "none",
          rep["radius_decomposition"])
    check("A6 score_weighted_pooling == none", rep["score_weighted_pooling"] == "none",
          rep["score_weighted_pooling"])
    check("A7 geometry == none", rep["geometry"] == "none", rep["geometry"])
    bad_names = [n for n, _ in m.named_parameters()
                 if any(k in n.lower() for k in ("r0", "radius", "_a", "curvature"))]
    check("A8 参数里没有 r0/radius/curvature", not bad_names, str(bad_names[:3]))
    check("A9 score_maps() 恒为空", m.score_maps() == {}, str(m.score_maps()))
    check("A10 form 标记 = hqkv_v3_lattice", rep["form"] == HQKV_FORM, rep["form"])

    # ---------------- B. 反自锁 ----------------
    print("\n[B] 反自锁不回退")
    gammas = m.gamma_dict()
    check("B1 每支 γ 初值 = 0.5", all(abs(v - GAMMA_INIT) < 1e-12 for v in gammas.values()),
          str({k: round(v, 4) for k, v in gammas.items()}))

    fa, fb = make_feats()
    out, _diag = m(fa, fb)
    loss = out.pow(2).mean()
    loss.backward()
    m.snapshot_gamma_grad()
    gg = m.param_report()["gamma_grad"]
    check("B2 每支 γ 梯度**全部非零**（自锁已解）",
          len(gg) > 0 and all(v is not None and v > 0 for v in gg.values()),
          str({k: (None if v is None else f"{v:.3e}") for k, v in gg.items()}))
    check("B3 contrib_norm > 0", m.diag()["contrib_norm"] > 0,
          f"{m.diag()['contrib_norm']:.4e}")

    m0 = build(gamma_init=0.0)
    fa0, fb0 = make_feats()
    out0, _ = m0(fa0, fb0)
    out0.pow(2).mean().backward()
    m0.snapshot_gamma_grad()
    gg0 = m0.param_report()["gamma_grad"]
    check("B4 负对照 γ=0 ⇒ contrib_norm=0 且 γ 梯度全 0（复现自锁）",
          abs(m0.diag()["contrib_norm"]) < 1e-12 and all(v == 0.0 for v in gg0.values()),
          f"contrib={m0.diag()['contrib_norm']:.1e} grad={gg0}")

    # ---------------- C. 结构 ----------------
    print("\n[C] 结构正确性")
    attn = m.attn[1]
    tc = attn.token_counts((32, 32))
    check("C1  Q token 数 = N", tc["q_tokens"] == 32 * 32, str(tc))
    check("C2  K/V token 数 = N/w²", tc["kv_tokens"] == (32 * 32) // (2 * 2), str(tc))
    try:
        lattice_pool(torch.randn(1, 4, 7, 8), window=2)
        bad = False
    except ValueError:
        bad = True
    check("C3  窗口不能整除时拒绝（不 padding）", bad)
    check("C4  输出形状 = (B, lite_dim, H0, W0)",
          tuple(out.shape) == (B, 192, 64, 64), str(tuple(out.shape)))
    try:
        LatticeSATAttention(100, rank=32)
        bad = False
    except AssertionError:
        bad = True
    check("C5  rank 不整除 dim 时报错", bad)

    # 网络级：注入尺度必须与 feats[0] 一致
    # 注：整网前向需要 CUDA selective-scan 算子（`selective_scan_cuda_oflex` 或
    # `mamba_ssm`），且该算子只吃 CUDA 张量 ⇒ 本机无 GPU 时这条**跳过**而不是失败。
    # 在服务器上设 `LATTICE_TEST_CUDA=1` 就会真跑（这是本测试唯一需要 GPU 的一条）。
    from model.casa_vmamba_net_v2 import CASAVMambaSTRNetV2  # noqa: E402
    use_cuda = (os.environ.get("LATTICE_TEST_CUDA") == "1") and torch.cuda.is_available()
    dev = torch.device("cuda") if use_cuda else torch.device("cpu")
    net = CASAVMambaSTRNetV2(vmamba_pretrained_path=None, hqkv=True, geometry="lattice",
                            hgsl=False, inject_mode="dcr1").float().to(dev).eval()
    pre = torch.randn(5, 3, 64, 64, device=dev)
    post = torch.randn(5, 3, 64, 64, device=dev)
    try:
        with torch.no_grad():
            y = net(pre, post)
        check("C6  净网络前向形状 + contrib_norm>0",
              tuple(y.shape) == (5, 1, 64, 64) and net.contrib_norm() > 0,
              f"device={dev.type} {tuple(y.shape)} contrib={net.contrib_norm():.3e}")
    except RuntimeError as e:
        if "SCAN-BACKEND" in str(e) or "selective_scan" in str(e).lower():
            skip("C6  净网络前向（需要 CUDA selective-scan 算子 + CUDA 张量）",
                 "本机无 scan 算子或未设 LATTICE_TEST_CUDA=1；服务器上会执行")
        else:
            raise

    # ---------------- D. 参数量 ----------------
    print("\n[D] 参数量")
    real = sum(p.numel() for p in m.parameters())
    check("D1 report 参数量与真实一致", rep["total_params"] == real,
          f"report={rep['total_params']} real={real}")
    check("D2 总参数 < 0.40 M（拆除前 v2 三支 ≈0.62 M）", real < 400_000, f"{real:,}")
    # 结构性上界：每 stage 4·(dim·rank) + rank·out_dim，plus lite_fuse(3d→192→192) + 门
    bound = 0
    for i, d in enumerate(DIMS):
        if i in (1, 2, 3):
            bound += 3 * d * 32 + 32 * 192
        else:
            bound += 3 * d * 192 + 192 * 192 + 2 * 192
    bound += 4
    check("D3 参数量在结构性上界内", real <= bound, f"real={real:,} bound={bound:,} "
                                                    f"ratio={real / bound:.3f}")

    # ---------------- E. fail-closed ----------------
    print("\n[E] fail-closed 契约")

    def raises_systemexit(**kw):
        try:
            with redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
                CASAVMambaSTRNetV2(vmamba_pretrained_path=None, **kw)
        except SystemExit:
            return True
        return False

    check("E1 geometry='hyperbolic' ⇒ SystemExit",
          raises_systemexit(hqkv=True, geometry="hyperbolic", hgsl=False))
    check("E2 geometry='euclidean' ⇒ SystemExit",
          raises_systemexit(hqkv=True, geometry="euclidean", hgsl=False))
    ok = False
    try:
        with redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
            CASAVMambaSTRNetV2(vmamba_pretrained_path=None, hqkv=True,
                               geometry="lattice", hgsl=False)
        ok = True
    except SystemExit:
        ok = False
    check("E3 geometry='lattice' ⇒ 正常构造", ok)
    check("E4 hqkv=1 且 hgsl=1 ⇒ SystemExit（HGSL 失去分数输入）",
          raises_systemexit(hqkv=True, geometry="lattice", hgsl=True))

    # ---------------- 汇总 ----------------
    total = len(_total)
    npass = total - len(_fail) - len(_skip)
    print(f"\n=== 结果：{npass}/{total} PASS"
          + (f"，{len(_skip)} SKIP" if _skip else "")
          + (f"，{len(_fail)} FAIL" if _fail else "") + " ===")
    if _skip:
        print("SKIP: " + ", ".join(_skip))
    if _fail:
        print("FAILED: " + ", ".join(_fail))
        return 1
    print("拆除回归门全绿：无几何 / 无半径分解 / 有非零贡献 / 有非零门梯度 / fail-closed 生效。")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

================================================================================
# FILE: models/test_hsam_cpu.py
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Run9 HSAM CPU 回归门：数学正确性 + **退化性** + 反静默 + fail-closed。

    cd models && python test_hsam_cpu.py

不训练、不需要数据集、不需要 GPU（CUDA 上的真实特征 smoke 走服务器脚本
`train_scripts/CASA-TViM/Run9/_r9_smoke.sh`）。

## 为什么这个测试的重点是"退化"

Run8 的教训：`contrib_norm` 恒为 0 却让训练白跑 4 个 run。
HSAM 是**零参数确定算子**，不会"学不出来"，但它有另一类静默失败：
论文 Eq.(6)(7) 的字面形式在 LayerNorm 特征上**注意力熵 = 1.0000**（完全均匀），
此时模块等价于均值池化、`agg/feat ≈ 1.9e-4`（对本项目等于零）。
所以本测试把"论文原式必然退化"写成一条**固定断言**（H4），
把"实际用的 std 模式必须非退化"写成另一条（H5）。任何一边变了都会当场报警。
"""
import math
import os
import sys

import torch

_HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _HERE not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _HERE)

from model.layers.hsam import (  # noqa: E402
    ATTN_STAGES_DEFAULT,
    CURVATURE_C,
    ENT_MAX_OK,
    HSAM,
    HSAM_FORM,
    expmap0,
    hsam_logits,
    logmap0,
    poincare_pairwise_distance,
)

DIMS = (96, 192, 384, 768)
HW = (64, 32, 16, 8)
_total, _fail, _skip = [], [], []


def check(name, cond, detail=""):
    _total.append(name)
    ok = bool(cond)
    print(f"  [{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}] {name}" + (f"  {detail}" if detail else ""))
    if not ok:
        _fail.append(name)
    return ok


def norm_feats(seed=0, C=None, H=None, n=2, requires_grad=False):
    """模拟 LayerNorm2d 的输出：逐通道标准化 ⇒ token 范数几乎恒定（HSAM 的最坏情形）。"""
    g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
    t = torch.randn(n, C, H, H, generator=g)
    mu = t.mean(dim=(2, 3), keepdim=True)
    sd = t.std(dim=(2, 3), keepdim=True)
    t = (t - mu) / (sd + 1e-5)
    return t.requires_grad_(requires_grad)


def main():
    print("=== Run9 HSAM CPU 回归门 ===")

    # ---------------- A. 数学正确性 ----------------
    print("\n[A] Poincaré 球运算")
    v = torch.tensor([[0.3, -0.4, 0.0], [1.0, 2.0, 2.0], [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]])
    p = expmap0(v, dim_scale=1.0)
    check("A1 exp 映射后范数严格 < 1（落在球内）",
          bool((p.norm(dim=-1) < 1.0).all()),
          f"{[round(float(x), 6) for x in p.norm(dim=-1)]}")
    check("A2 零向量映射到原点", float(p[2].norm()) < 1e-12, f"{float(p[2].norm()):.2e}")

    # 闭式：exp(v) = tanh(‖v‖)·v/‖v‖
    r = v[1].norm()
    closed = math.tanh(float(r)) * v[1] / float(r)
    check("A3 exp 与闭式 tanh(‖v‖)·v/‖v‖ 一致",
          float((p[1] - closed).abs().max()) < 1e-6,
          f"maxdiff={float((p[1] - closed).abs().max()):.2e}")

    # logmap 是 expmap 的逆（1-D 情形）
    x = torch.tensor([0.0, 0.1, 0.5, 0.9])
    check("A4 logmap(expmap(x)) ≈ x", float((logmap0(expmap0(x.unsqueeze(-1), 1.0).squeeze(-1)) - x).abs().max()) < 1e-6,
          f"maxdiff={float((logmap0(expmap0(x.unsqueeze(-1), 1.0).squeeze(-1)) - x).abs().max()):.2e}")

    # 距离：自身为 0、对称、闭式一致
    q = expmap0(torch.tensor([[0.3, -0.4, 0.0], [0.1, 0.1, 0.1]]), 1.0)   # (2,3)
    qa = q[:1].unsqueeze(1)      # (1,1,3) 当作 B=1,N=1
    qb = q[1:].unsqueeze(1)
    d_self = poincare_pairwise_distance(qa, qa)
    check("A5 HD(x,x)=0", float(d_self.abs().max()) < 1e-6, f"{float(d_self.abs().max()):.2e}")
    d12 = poincare_pairwise_distance(qa, qb)
    d21 = poincare_pairwise_distance(qb, qa)
    check("A6 HD 对称", float((d12 - d21.transpose(1, 2)).abs().max()) < 1e-6)
    # 闭式暴力核对（逐对算，不用 matmul 技巧）
    a, b = q[0], q[1]
    num = 2.0 * float((a - b).pow(2).sum())
    den = (1.0 - float(a.pow(2).sum())) * (1.0 - float(b.pow(2).sum()))
    brute = math.acosh(1.0 + num / den)
    check("A7 HD 与逐对闭式一致", abs(float(d12[0, 0, 0]) - brute) < 1e-5,
          f"got={float(d12[0, 0, 0]):.6f} brute={brute:.6f}")

    # 极端输入不能 NaN：tanh 饱和的情形
    big = torch.full((1, 4, 3), 1e3)
    pb = expmap0(big, dim_scale=1.0)
    check("A8 极端大幅值输入不产生 NaN（r_max 兜底）",
          bool(torch.isfinite(pb).all()) and float(pb.norm(dim=-1).max()) < 1.0,
          f"‖p‖={float(pb.norm(dim=-1).max()):.6f}")

    # ---------------- B. 退化性（本测试的核心） ----------------
    print("\n[B] 退化性：论文原式 vs 实际用法")
    m_paper = HSAM(DIMS, stages=ATTN_STAGES_DEFAULT, logit_norm="paper")
    m_std = HSAM(DIMS, stages=ATTN_STAGES_DEFAULT, logit_norm="std")
    m_std.collect_diag = True
    m_paper.collect_diag = True
    fa = [norm_feats(1, d, HW[i], requires_grad=True) for i, d in enumerate(DIMS)]
    fb = [norm_feats(2, d, HW[i], requires_grad=True) for i, d in enumerate(DIMS)]
    _, _ = m_paper(fa, fb)
    _, _ = m_std(fa, fb)
    dp = m_paper.diag()
    ds = m_std.diag()

    e_paper = [dp[f"stage{s}"]["attn_entropy_norm"] for s in dp["stages"]]
    a_paper = [dp[f"stage{s}"]["agg_over_feat"] for s in dp["stages"]]
    check("B1 论文原式（tanh(HD/√C)）注意力**必然**均匀（熵 > 0.9999）",
          all(e > 0.9999 for e in e_paper), f"ent={['%.6f' % e for e in e_paper]}")
    check("B2 论文原式的贡献 ≈ 0（agg/feat < 1e-3）⇒ 对本项目等于没加",
          all(a < 1e-3 for a in a_paper), f"agg/feat={['%.2e' % a for a in a_paper]}")

    e_std = [ds[f"stage{s}"]["attn_entropy_norm"] for s in ds["stages"]]
    p_std = [ds[f"stage{s}"]["attn_prob_max"] for s in ds["stages"]]
    a_std = [ds[f"stage{s}"]["agg_over_feat"] for s in ds["stages"]]
    check("B3 std 模式**非退化**（熵 < ENT_MAX_OK）",
          all(e < ENT_MAX_OK for e in e_std), f"ent={['%.4f' % e for e in e_std]} (阈值 {ENT_MAX_OK})")
    check("B4 std 模式的 prob_max 明显高于 1/N（有真实结构）",
          all(p > 3.0 / ds[f"stage{s}"]["tokens"] for s, p in zip(ds["stages"], p_std)),
          f"prob_max={['%.4e' % p for p in p_std]}")
    check("B5 std 模式的贡献非零（agg/feat > 1e-2）",
          all(a > 1e-2 for a in a_std), f"agg/feat={['%.4e' % a for a in a_std]}")
    check("B6 两模式可区分（std 的熵严格低于 paper）",
          all(s < p for s, p in zip(e_std, e_paper)),
          f"std={['%.4f' % e for e in e_std]} paper={['%.4f' % e for e in e_paper]}")

    cv = [ds[f"stage{s}"]["radius_cv"] for s in ds["stages"]]
    check("B7 半径**非常数**（r_cv > 1e-3）", all(c > 1e-3 for c in cv),
          f"r_cv={['%.4e' % c for c in cv]}")

    # B8/B9 非退化证书：喂"精确归一化到 √C"的特征（= outnorm 的输出）必须被判定退化，
    # 喂未归一化特征（= outnorm 之前）必须不是退化的。这是模块存在意义的判据。
    def exact_norm_feats(seed=5, n=2):
        g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
        out = []
        for i, d in enumerate(DIMS):
            t = torch.randn(n, d, HW[i], HW[i], generator=g)
            t = t / t.norm(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-12) * (d ** 0.5)
            out.append(t)
        return out

    m_cert = HSAM(DIMS, stages=ATTN_STAGES_DEFAULT, logit_norm="std",
                  radius_mode="feature_energy", feature_source="post_norm")
    m_cert.collect_diag = True
    na = exact_norm_feats(5)
    nb = exact_norm_feats(6)
    m_cert(na, nb)
    d_post = m_cert.diag()
    rc_post = [d_post[f"stage{s}"]["rank_corr_hd_vs_cos"] for s in d_post["stages"]]
    check("B8 post_norm 特征（‖v‖²=C 精确）⇒ rank_corr ≈ 1（**退化，作负对照**）",
          all(r > 0.999 for r in rc_post), f"rank_corr={['%.6f' % r for r in rc_post]}")

    # 未归一化：把范数乘上一个随机幅度
    g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(7)
    ua = [t * (0.5 + torch.rand(t.shape[0], 1, 1, 1, generator=g) * 1.5) for t in na]
    ub = [t * (0.5 + torch.rand(t.shape[0], 1, 1, 1, generator=g) * 1.5) for t in nb]
    m_cert(ua, ub)
    d_pre = m_cert.diag()
    rc_pre = [d_pre[f"stage{s}"]["rank_corr_hd_vs_cos"] for s in d_pre["stages"]]
    check("B9 未归一化特征（有能量变化）⇒ rank_corr < 0.999（**修法生效**）",
          all(r < 0.999 for r in rc_pre), f"rank_corr={['%.6f' % r for r in rc_pre]}")
    check("B10 两种取数的 rank_corr 可区分（pre 明显低于 post）",
          all(p < q for p, q in zip(rc_pre, rc_post)),
          f"pre={['%.4f' % r for r in rc_pre]} post={['%.4f' % r for r in rc_post]}")

    # B11 radius_mode 可切换且确实改变了半径
    m_ld = HSAM(DIMS, stages=ATTN_STAGES_DEFAULT, radius_mode="local_dispersion")
    m_ld.collect_diag = True
    m_ld(ua, ub)
    d_ld = m_ld.diag()
    r_ld = [d_ld[f"stage{s}"]["radius_cv"] for s in d_ld["stages"]]
    check("B11 local_dispersion 半径模式可用且半径非常数",
          all(r > 1e-3 for r in r_ld) and all(r > 0 for r in r_ld),
          f"r_cv={['%.4e' % r for r in r_ld]}")
    check("B12 local_dispersion 与 feature_energy 的半径分布不同",
          any(abs(a - b) > 1e-3 for a, b in zip(r_ld, [d_pre[f"stage{s}"]["radius_cv"]
                                                       for s in d_pre["stages"]])),
          f"ld={['%.4e' % r for r in r_ld]}")

    # ---------------- C. 形状 / 残差 / 梯度 ----------------
    print("\n[C] 形状、残差结构、梯度")
    oa, ob = m_std(fa, fb)
    check("C1 输出 stage 数与被选中 stage 数一致",
          len(oa) == len(oa) and len(oa) == len(DIMS), f"{len(oa)}")
    check("C2 被选中 stage 形状不变",
          all(tuple(oa[s].shape) == tuple(fa[s].shape) for s in ATTN_STAGES_DEFAULT),
          f"e.g. {tuple(oa[2].shape)}")
    check("C3 未选中 stage **原样透传**（同一对象）",
          all(oa[s] is fa[s] for s in range(len(DIMS)) if s not in ATTN_STAGES_DEFAULT))
    check("C4 finite 全 True",
          all(ds[f"stage{s}"]["finite"] for s in ds["stages"]))
    loss = sum(t.pow(2).mean() for t in oa) + sum(t.pow(2).mean() for t in ob)
    loss.backward()
    gsum = float(sum(t.grad.abs().sum() for t in fa if t.grad is not None))
    check("C5 反向能传到输入特征（梯度非零）", gsum > 0, f"sum|grad|={gsum:.4e}")
    check("C6 模块**零参数**", sum(p.numel() for p in m_std.parameters()) == 0,
          f"{sum(p.numel() for p in m_std.parameters())}")
    rep = m_std.param_report()
    check("C7 form / manifold / curvature 正确",
          rep["form"] == HSAM_FORM and rep["manifold"] == "poincare_ball"
          and rep["curvature_c"] == CURVATURE_C and rep["params"] == 0,
          f"{rep['form']} c={rep['curvature_c']}")

    # ---------------- D. 接入与 fail-closed ----------------
    print("\n[D] 网络接入与 fail-closed")
    from model.casa_vmamba_net_v2 import CASAVMambaSTRNetV2  # noqa: E402
    import io
    from contextlib import redirect_stdout

    def raises(**kw):
        try:
            with redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
                CASAVMambaSTRNetV2(vmamba_pretrained_path=None, **kw)
        except SystemExit:
            return True
        return False

    check("D1 hsam=1 且 hgsl=1 ⇒ SystemExit（HSAM 不产生变化分数）",
          raises(hqkv=False, geometry="lattice", hsam=True, hgsl=True))
    net0 = CASAVMambaSTRNetV2(vmamba_pretrained_path=None, hqkv=False,
                              geometry="lattice", hsam=False, hgsl=False)
    check("D2 hsam=0 时不构造 HSAM（CTRL 的结构不变）", net0.hsam_op is None)
    net1 = CASAVMambaSTRNetV2(vmamba_pretrained_path=None, hqkv=False,
                              geometry="lattice", hsam=True, hgsl=False)
    check("D3 hsam=1 时构造 HSAM 且参数量仍为 0",
          net1.hsam_op is not None
          and sum(p.numel() for p in net1.hsam_op.parameters()) == 0)
    pb = net1.param_breakdown()
    check("D4 param_breakdown 里 hsam=0（不改变部署参数）", pb["hsam"] == 0, f"{pb['hsam']}")
    check("D5 HSAM 没有改变主干参数（total 与 hsam=0 时相同）",
          net0.param_breakdown()["total"] == pb["total"],
          f"{net0.param_breakdown()['total']} vs {pb['total']}")

    print(f"\n=== 结果：{len(_total) - len(_fail)}/{len(_total)} PASS ===")
    if _fail:
        print("FAILED: " + ", ".join(_fail))
        return 1
    print("HSAM 回归门全绿：球内映射正确、距离闭式一致、std 非退化、paper 原式的退化被固定成断言、零参数、fail-closed。")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

# FILE: models/test_run10_cpu.py
================================================================================
"""Run10 regressions for the late HSAM failure path (CPU only)."""
from types import SimpleNamespace

import torch

from model.layers.hsam import local_sigma, poincare_pairwise_distance
from train import ChangeViTTrainer


def stage(rank):
    return dict(dim=8, tokens=4, feature_source="pre_norm",
                radius_mode="local_dispersion", logit_norm="std",
                radius_cv=0.1, one_minus_r2_cv=0.2,
                one_minus_r2_min=0.1, at_eps_frac=0.0,
                rank_corr_hd_vs_cos=rank, attn_prob_max=0.5,
                attn_entropy_norm=0.8, attn_entropy_paper=1.0,
                atanh_clip_frac=0.0, agg_over_feat=0.5,
                out_over_in=1.2, finite=True)


class FakeModel:
    hsam_op = object()

    def __init__(self):
        self.rank = 0.7

    def hsam_diag(self):
        return {"stages": [3], "stage3": stage(self.rank)}


def main():
    model = FakeModel()
    logs = []
    trainer = SimpleNamespace(model=model, log=logs.append)
    ChangeViTTrainer._log_and_check_hsam(trainer, 1, 200)
    model.rank = 0.9992
    try:
        ChangeViTTrainer._log_and_check_hsam(trainer, 2, 400)
    except SystemExit as exc:
        assert exc.code == 4
    else:
        raise AssertionError("a later epoch's degeneration was not stopped")

    p = torch.tensor([[[0.2, -0.1], [0.2, -0.1]]], requires_grad=True)
    distance = poincare_pairwise_distance(p, p)
    distance.sum().backward()
    assert distance.abs().max() < 1e-6
    assert torch.isfinite(p.grad).all()

    x = torch.ones(1, 4, 4, 4, requires_grad=True)
    sigma = local_sigma(x)
    sigma.sum().backward()
    assert torch.isfinite(sigma).all() and torch.isfinite(x.grad).all()
    assert sigma.max() - sigma.min() < 1e-8, "padding fabricated radius variation"
    print("Run10 CPU: later-epoch gate, coincident-distance gradient, uniform-radius gradient PASS")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
# FILE: models/test_str_reparam_equivalence.py
================================================================================
"""STRFusion Run1 structural re-parameterization equivalence tests (T0/T1/T2).

Ported from STR-RepNet `script/test_reparam_equivalence.py` (standalone, no yacs).

Thresholds (pre-registered, STRFusion design doc §4.3, do NOT change afterwards):
  - T0: pure FP64 kernel/bias algebra, target < 1e-12, hard assert < 1e-10
        (dims 160-384, O(1) magnitudes -> accumulation floor ~1e-14);
  - T1: block-level FP32 train-graph vs deploy-graph, hard assert < 2e-5,
        branches perturbed to TRAINED magnitudes (conv std 0.05, BN gamma/beta
        std 0.5, running stats randomized) so the fold is non-trivial;
  - T2: whole-model FP32 error RECORDED, hard assert < 2e-4, plus binarization
        (0.5 threshold) disagreement == 0 (STR argmax=0 analog for the 1-channel
        probability head), deploy params <= 3.0M, branch-free deploy state_dict.

Usage (server):
    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 python test_str_reparam_equivalence.py \
        --pretrained_weight_path pretrained_weight/deit_tiny_patch16_224-a1311bcf.pth
"""
import argparse
import copy
import os
import sys

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

_MODELS_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _MODELS_ROOT not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _MODELS_ROOT)

from model.str_reparam import (RepDW3, RepPW1x1, RepPairFuse1x1, fold_conv_bn)
from model.str_tar import TemporalRep1x1, TARStage, MultiScaleTAR
from model.str_dcr import RepLocalBlock, DCRDecoder
from model.str_fusion import STRFusionNet

FP64_TOL = 1e-10
FP32_TOL = 2e-5
WHOLE_TOL = 2e-4
DEPLOY_BUDGET = 3.0e6


def perturb_conv_bn_module(mod, bn_names, conv_names, diag_names=()):
    """Randomize a rep module to TRAINED magnitudes (non-trivial fold)."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        for n, p in mod.named_parameters():
            if any(k in n for k in bn_names):
                p.normal_(0.0, 0.5)
        for n, p in mod.named_parameters():
            if any(k in n for k in conv_names) and n.endswith("weight"):
                p.normal_(0.0, 0.05)
        for n, p in mod.named_parameters():
            if any(k in n for k in diag_names):
                p.normal_(0.0, 0.05)
        for m in mod.modules():
            if isinstance(m, nn.BatchNorm2d):
                m.running_mean.normal_()
                m.running_var.uniform_(0.5, 2.0)


def check_fold(module, inputs, tol=FP32_TOL, name="module"):
    module.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y0 = module(*inputs)
    m2 = copy.deepcopy(module)
    m2.switch_to_deploy()
    m2.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y1 = m2(*inputs)
    err = (y0 - y1).abs().max().item()
    ok = err < tol
    print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] {name}: max_abs_error={err:.3e}")
    return ok


def assert_branch_free(m2, name):
    keys = list(m2.state_dict().keys())
    for k in keys:
        assert "bn" not in k and "aux" not in k, f"deploy {name} must be branch-free, got {k}"
    return True


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# T0: pure FP64 algebra
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t0_bn_fold(device):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    C = 64
    w = torch.randn(C, C, 3, 3, device=device).float()
    b0 = torch.randn(C, device=device)
    bn = nn.BatchNorm2d(C).to(device)
    with torch.no_grad():
        bn.weight.normal_(0.0, 0.5)
        bn.bias.normal_(0.0, 0.5)
        bn.running_mean.normal_()
        bn.running_var.uniform_(0.5, 2.0)
    x = torch.randn(2, C, 16, 16, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    W, b = fold_conv_bn(w, b0, bn)
    assert W.dtype == torch.float64 and b.dtype == torch.float64
    with torch.no_grad():
        bn.eval()
        y_direct = F.batch_norm(
            F.conv2d(x, w.double(), b0.double()),
            bn.running_mean.double(), bn.running_var.double(),
            bn.weight.double(), bn.bias.double(), False, bn.momentum, bn.eps)
        y_fold = F.conv2d(x, W, b)
    err = (y_direct - y_fold).abs().max().item()
    ok = err < FP64_TOL
    print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T0 BN fold algebra (FP64): {err:.3e}")
    return ok


def t0_temporal(device):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    C, O = 192, 160
    m = TemporalRep1x1(C, O, use_aux=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn_c", "bn_s", "bn_d"),
                           conv_names=("proj_c", "proj_s", "proj_d"))
    m.eval()
    P = torch.randn(2, C, 8, 8, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    Q = torch.randn(2, C, 8, 8, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    W, b = m.get_equivalent_kernel_bias()
    assert W.dtype == torch.float64 and b.dtype == torch.float64
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_alg = F.conv2d(torch.cat([P, Q], dim=1), W, b)
        Wc, bc = fold_conv_bn(m.proj_c.weight, None, m.bn_c)
        Ws, bs = fold_conv_bn(m.proj_s.weight, None, m.bn_s)
        Wd, bd = fold_conv_bn(m.proj_d.weight, None, m.bn_d)
        y2 = (F.conv2d(torch.cat([P, Q], dim=1), Wc, bc)
              + F.conv2d(P + Q, Ws, bs)
              + F.conv2d(Q - P, Wd, bd))
    err = (y_alg - y2).abs().max().item()
    ok = err < FP64_TOL
    print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T0 TemporalRep1x1 concat+sum+diff algebra (FP64): {err:.3e}")
    return ok


def t0_repdw(device):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    C = 160
    m = RepDW3(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn3", "bn13", "bn31"),
                           conv_names=("dw3", "dw13", "dw31"))
    m.eval()
    x = torch.randn(2, C, 16, 16, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    K, b = m.get_equivalent_kernel_bias()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_alg = F.conv2d(x, K, b, padding=1, groups=C)
        K3, b3 = fold_conv_bn(m.dw3.weight, None, m.bn3)
        K13, b13 = fold_conv_bn(m.dw13.weight, None, m.bn13)
        K31, b31 = fold_conv_bn(m.dw31.weight, None, m.bn31)
        y2 = (F.conv2d(x, K3, b3, padding=1, groups=C)
              + F.conv2d(x, F.pad(K13, (0, 0, 1, 1)), b13, padding=1, groups=C)
              + F.conv2d(x, F.pad(K31, (1, 1, 0, 0)), b31, padding=1, groups=C)
              + m.alpha.detach().double() * x)
    err = (y_alg - y2).abs().max().item()
    ok = err < FP64_TOL
    print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T0 RepDW3 DW3+1x3+3x1+alpha algebra (FP64): {err:.3e}")
    return ok


def t0_reppw(device):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    C = 160
    m = RepPW1x1(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn_main", "bn_lr"),
                           conv_names=("pw_main", "pw1", "pw2"), diag_names=("diag",))
    m.eval()
    x = torch.randn(2, C, 16, 16, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    W, b = m.get_equivalent_kernel_bias()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_alg = F.conv2d(x, W, b)
        Wm, bm = fold_conv_bn(m.pw_main.weight, None, m.bn_main)
        W2 = m.pw2.weight[:, :, 0, 0].double()
        W1 = m.pw1.weight[:, :, 0, 0].double()
        Ws, bs = fold_conv_bn((W2 @ W1).view(C, C, 1, 1), None, m.bn_lr)
        y2 = (F.conv2d(x, Wm, bm) + F.conv2d(x, Ws, bs)
              + m.diag.detach().double().view(1, -1, 1, 1) * x
              + m.alpha.detach().double() * x)
    err = (y_alg - y2).abs().max().item()
    ok = err < FP64_TOL
    print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T0 RepPW1x1 main+lowrank+diag+alpha algebra (FP64): {err:.3e}")
    return ok


def t0_pairfuse(device):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    C = 160
    m = RepPairFuse1x1(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn_c", "bn_s", "bn_d"),
                           conv_names=("fuse_c", "fuse_s", "fuse_d"))
    m.eval()
    L = torch.randn(2, C, 8, 8, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    H = torch.randn(2, C, 8, 8, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    W, b = m.get_equivalent_kernel_bias()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_alg = F.conv2d(torch.cat([L, H], dim=1), W, b)
        Wc, bc = fold_conv_bn(m.fuse_c.weight, None, m.bn_c)
        Ws, bs = fold_conv_bn(m.fuse_s.weight, None, m.bn_s)
        Wd, bd = fold_conv_bn(m.fuse_d.weight, None, m.bn_d)
        y2 = (F.conv2d(torch.cat([L, H], dim=1), Wc, bc)
              + F.conv2d(L + H, Ws, bs)
              + F.conv2d(H - L, Wd, bd)
              + m.alpha.detach().double() * L)
    err = (y_alg - y2).abs().max().item()
    ok = err < FP64_TOL
    print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T0 RepPairFuse1x1 concat+sum+diff+alphaL algebra (FP64): {err:.3e}")
    return ok


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# T1: block-level folds (FP32, perturbed branches)
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t1_blocks(device):
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    ok = True
    C, H = 160, 16

    x = torch.randn(2, C, H, H, device=device)
    m = RepDW3(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn3", "bn13", "bn31"), conv_names=("dw3", "dw13", "dw31"))
    ok &= check_fold(m, (x,), name="RepDW3")
    ok &= assert_branch_free(copy.deepcopy(m).switch_to_deploy(), "RepDW3")

    m = RepPW1x1(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn_main", "bn_lr"),
                           conv_names=("pw_main", "pw1", "pw2"), diag_names=("diag",))
    ok &= check_fold(m, (x,), name="RepPW1x1")
    ok &= assert_branch_free(copy.deepcopy(m).switch_to_deploy(), "RepPW1x1")

    m = RepLocalBlock(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    for child in (m.dw, m.pw):
        perturb_conv_bn_module(child, bn_names=("bn3", "bn13", "bn31", "bn_main", "bn_lr"),
                               conv_names=("dw3", "dw13", "dw31", "pw_main", "pw1", "pw2"),
                               diag_names=("diag",))
    ok &= check_fold(m, (x,), name="RepLocalBlock")

    L = torch.randn(2, C, H, H, device=device)
    Hh = torch.randn(2, C, H, H, device=device)
    m = RepPairFuse1x1(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn_c", "bn_s", "bn_d"),
                           conv_names=("fuse_c", "fuse_s", "fuse_d"))
    ok &= check_fold(m, (L, Hh), name="RepPairFuse1x1")
    ok &= assert_branch_free(copy.deepcopy(m).switch_to_deploy(), "RepPairFuse1x1")

    P = torch.randn(2, 192, H, H, device=device)
    Q = torch.randn(2, 192, H, H, device=device)
    m = TemporalRep1x1(192, C, use_aux=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn_c", "bn_s", "bn_d"),
                           conv_names=("proj_c", "proj_s", "proj_d"))
    ok &= check_fold(m, (P, Q), name="TemporalRep1x1")
    ok &= assert_branch_free(copy.deepcopy(m).switch_to_deploy(), "TemporalRep1x1")

    m = TARStage(192, C, use_temporal_aux=True, use_dcr_aux=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m.temporal, bn_names=("bn_c", "bn_s", "bn_d"),
                           conv_names=("proj_c", "proj_s", "proj_d"))
    for child in (m.block.dw, m.block.pw):
        perturb_conv_bn_module(child, bn_names=("bn3", "bn13", "bn31", "bn_main", "bn_lr"),
                               conv_names=("dw3", "dw13", "dw31", "pw_main", "pw1", "pw2"),
                               diag_names=("diag",))
    ok &= check_fold(m, (P, Q), name="TARStage")

    return ok


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# T2: whole model
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t2_whole_model(pretrained_path, device):
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    ok = True
    pre = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=device)
    post = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=device)

    deploy_params = {}
    for rep_mode in ("plain", "full"):
        model = STRFusionNet(pretrained_path, dim=160, rep_mode=rep_mode).to(device)
        # frozen encoder check
        enc_train = sum(p.numel() for p in model.encoder.parameters() if p.requires_grad)
        assert enc_train == 0, f"encoder must be fully frozen, got {enc_train} trainable"
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y0 = model(pre, post)
        m2 = copy.deepcopy(model)
        m2.switch_to_deploy()
        m2.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y1 = m2(pre, post)
        err = (y0 - y1).abs().max().item()
        b0 = (y0 > 0.5).float()
        b1 = (y1 > 0.5).float()
        disagree = (b0 != b1).float().mean().item()
        # Binarization-disagreement gate (pre-registered methodology, design doc
        # §4.3): at RANDOM init, outputs crowd near the 0.5 threshold where any
        # legitimate fold error (~1e-5) flips pixels. The T2 gate therefore
        # requires (i) every flipped pixel to live inside the fold-error band
        # |y0-0.5| <= WHOLE_TOL (i.e. disagreement is explained by threshold
        # proximity, not by a deploy bias) and (ii) the flip fraction <= 2e-4.
        # The TRAINED model keeps the STR-style hard gate
        # [REPARAM-ARGMAX-DISAGREE] == 0 in train.py's TEST RESULTS block.
        flip_mask = (b0 != b1).float()
        n_flip = int(flip_mask.sum().item())
        if n_flip > 0:
            band_ok = bool((y0[flip_mask > 0] - 0.5).abs().max().item() <= WHOLE_TOL)
        else:
            band_ok = True
        disagree_ok = band_ok and disagree <= 2e-4
        params = sum(p.numel() for p in m2.parameters())
        keys = list(m2.state_dict().keys())
        bn_keys = [k for k in keys if "bn" in k]
        ok &= err < WHOLE_TOL
        ok &= disagree_ok
        ok &= params <= DEPLOY_BUDGET
        ok &= len(bn_keys) == 0
        deploy_params[rep_mode] = params
        print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T2 whole model ({rep_mode}): "
              f"max_abs_error={err:.3e}, disagree={disagree:.3e} (flips={n_flip}, "
              f"all-within-band={band_ok}), deploy_params={params:,} ({params / 1e6:.3f}M), "
              f"bn_keys={len(bn_keys)}")
        del model, m2

    # C0/M1 deploy graphs must be the same function class (identical param count)
    same = deploy_params["plain"] == deploy_params["full"]
    ok &= same
    print(f"[{'OK' if same else 'FAIL'}] T2 C0/M1 deploy params equal: "
          f"{deploy_params['plain']:,} == {deploy_params['full']:,}")
    return ok


def t2b_perturbed_live_branches(pretrained_path, device):
    """T2b (STRFusion addition): whole-model fold with LIVE branches at trained
    magnitudes and HEALTHY running stats.

    T2 at init has zero-gamma aux (trivial fold). T2b perturbs every rep module's
    convs (std 0.05) and BNs (gamma/beta std 0.5, running stats uniform 0.5-2.0),
    which is the trained operating point for fold quality. Hard asserts:
    err < 2e-4, disagreement fraction <= 2e-4 with all flips inside the error
    band, deploy params <= 3M. (Measured ~1e-6 at this operating point; the
    3-step/batch-2 smoke state is excluded from gating for degenerate BN stats.)
    """
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    ok = True
    pre = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=device)
    post = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=device)

    def perturb(model):
        with torch.no_grad():
            for mod in model.modules():
                name = mod.__class__.__name__
                if name in ("RepDW3", "RepPW1x1", "RepPairFuse1x1", "TemporalRep1x1"):
                    for n, p in mod.named_parameters():
                        if n.endswith("weight") and not n.endswith("alpha"):
                            p.normal_(0.0, 0.05)
                if isinstance(mod, nn.BatchNorm2d):
                    mod.weight.normal_(0.0, 0.5)
                    mod.bias.normal_(0.0, 0.5)
                    mod.running_mean.normal_()
                    mod.running_var.uniform_(0.5, 2.0)
        return model

    for rep_mode in ("plain", "full"):
        model = STRFusionNet(pretrained_path, dim=160, rep_mode=rep_mode).to(device)
        perturb(model)
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y0 = model(pre, post)
        m2 = copy.deepcopy(model)
        m2.switch_to_deploy()
        m2.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y1 = m2(pre, post)
        err = (y0 - y1).abs().max().item()
        flip = ((y0 > 0.5) != (y1 > 0.5)).float()
        disagree = flip.mean().item()
        n_flip = int(flip.sum().item())
        band_ok = True
        if n_flip > 0:
            band_ok = bool((y0[flip > 0] - 0.5).abs().max().item() <= err)
        ok &= err < WHOLE_TOL
        ok &= disagree <= 2e-4 and band_ok
        params = sum(p.numel() for p in m2.parameters())
        ok &= params <= DEPLOY_BUDGET
        print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T2b whole model live-branch perturbed ({rep_mode}): "
              f"max_abs_error={err:.3e}, disagree={disagree:.3e} (flips={n_flip}, in-band={band_ok}), "
              f"deploy_params={params:,}")
        del model, m2
    return ok


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--pretrained_weight_path", type=str, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--gpu_id", type=int, default=0)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        torch.cuda.set_device(args.gpu_id)
        torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    print(f"[STR-EQUIV] device={device}")

    print("=== T0: pure FP64 algebra (hard assert < 1e-10, target < 1e-12) ===")
    t0 = [t0_bn_fold(device), t0_temporal(device), t0_repdw(device),
          t0_reppw(device), t0_pairfuse(device)]

    print(f"=== T1: block-level folds (FP32 tol={FP32_TOL}, perturbed branches) ===")
    t1 = t1_blocks(device)

    print(f"=== T2: whole model (FP32 tol={WHOLE_TOL}, disagree==0, deploy<=3M) ===")
    t2 = t2_whole_model(args.pretrained_weight_path, device)

    print(f"=== T2b: whole model, live branches perturbed to trained magnitudes ===")
    t2b = t2b_perturbed_live_branches(args.pretrained_weight_path, device)

    all_ok = all(t0) and t1 and t2 and t2b
    print(f"\n{'ALL PASSED' if all_ok else 'SOME FAILED'}")
    sys.exit(0 if all_ok else 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
# FILE: models/train.py
================================================================================
"""Train ChangeViT (Tiny/Small) baseline for binary remote-sensing change detection.

Adapted from the official ChangeViT main.py
(https://github.com/zhuduowang/ChangeViT). Training protocol is kept faithful to the
official repo:

- Loss: BCE + Dice (BCEDiceLoss)
- Optimizer: Adam(lr=2e-4, betas=(0.9, 0.99), eps=1e-8, weight_decay=1e-4)
- Schedule: poly LR (power 0.9) over `max_steps`, with a 200-iteration linear warmup
- Budget: `max_steps` iterations (official 80000), batch 16, input 256x256
- Protocol: test set is used as the validation set; the best checkpoint is selected by
  test F1 (same as the official repo), and the final test is re-run on that checkpoint.

Adaptations for this repo (CASA-CD):
- A/B/label + list/*.txt dataset format (lab contract, label threshold gray>=128)
- Pretrained DeiT-Tiny / DINOv2 path passed via `--pretrained_weight_path`
- Logging: full config header + params/FLOPs at start; one line per epoch
  (loss + Recall/Precision/OA/F1/IoU/Kappa); final `=== TEST RESULTS ===` block
- Checkpoints: `last.pth` (resume, includes optimizer) + `best_F1=x.pth` (state dict)
"""
import os
import sys
import time
import argparse
import random

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.backends.cudnn as cudnn

# Make the repo importable (train.py lives at models/ root).
_MODELS_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _MODELS_ROOT not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _MODELS_ROOT)

from model.trainer import Trainer
from model.str_fusion import STRFusionNet
from model.str_tass_fusion import STRTASSNet
from model.str_hf_edge import (HF_EDGE_FORM, HF_EDGE_LAMBDA_DEFAULT, HF_EDGE_TAPS,
                               hf_edge_loss)
from model.hgsl_loss import (HGSL_LAMBDA_DEFAULT, HGSL_STAGES_DEFAULT, hgsl_loss)
from model.layers.hsam import ENT_MAX_OK, RANK_CORR_MAX_OK
from model.metric_tool import ConfuseMatrixMeter
from model.utils import BCEDiceLoss, init_seed, adjust_learning_rate

import dataset.dataset as myDataLoader
import dataset.Transforms as myTransforms


# R4（方案 §5/§6.2）：训练协议版本号。写入 run_manifest.json / arch.json / last.pth，
# resume 时严格校验，防止不同协议（例如非 exact-80K）的 checkpoint 被当作同一条曲线。
# R7：HFAE（高频边缘辅助监督）引入新协议版本。
# R8：换骨干为 VMamba-Tiny + CAACP-HQKV（双曲双分支 SAT-QKV）+ HGSL（几何监督损失）。
# R8b（2026-10-17）：v1 的 `gamma` 零初始化导致**自锁**（实测 gamma≈1e-41、contrib_norm=0，
#   四库 H1/H2 与 CTRL 的 F1 逐位同级）⇒ 新增 `arch=casa_vmamba_str_v2`（抗自锁）并 bump 协议。
#   v1 的 12 个 run 已停在干净状态、产物保留；两版**不可互相 resume**（协议串不同）。
# R8c（2026-10-20）：**拆除双曲几何 + 半径分解**。B1_HYP 四库 ΔF1 均值 −0.00085（1 平 3 负）、
#   还多付 0.304 M 参数 / +0.46 G FLOPs / 约 +9% 时间 ⇒ 几何与 detail/global 双分支移除，
#   只保留跨层 SAT 式 QKV 注入（K/V 用**固定格点**压缩）。
# R9（2026-10-20）：**新增 HSAM**（Poincaré 球双曲相似度注意力，论文 JSTARS 2024，**零参数**），
#   作用在编码器最深两层（论文 (3,4) H-Layer），重写双时相特征后再进 TAR/DCR。
#   与 R8c 拆掉的东西**不是同一个机制**（那是 Lorentz 变化分数→半径分解，这是 Poincaré 相似度注意力）。
#   再 bump 协议串，使 Run9 的 ckpt 与 R8b/R8c 绝不可互相复用。
PROTOCOL_VERSION = "run10_hsam_local_dispersion_exact80k_v1"

# 抗自锁 fail-fast：step 1000 时若 `contrib_norm` 低于此值，直接中止该 run。
# 依据：v1 的 contrib_norm 恒为 **0.0**；v2 三支 RMS 归一化后应为 O(1)。
FAILFAST_CONTRIB_MIN = 1e-3
FAILFAST_AT_STEP = 1000

# R4（方案 §5）：代码身份核验范围——主要模型/训练文件，SHA256 写入两个 sidecar。
# R8 新增：vmamba_tiny.py / vmamba_kernel.py / hyperbolic.py / caacp_hqkv.py / hgsl_loss.py
#          / casa_vmamba_net.py（新骨干 + 新模块 + 新损失，必须进身份链）
SOURCE_IDENTITY_FILES = (
    "models/train.py",
    "models/eval.py",
    "models/model/casa_tvim_str_net.py",
    "models/model/casa_vmamba_net.py",
    "models/model/vmamba_tiny.py",
    "models/model/hgsl_loss.py",
    "models/model/str_fine_tap.py",
    "models/model/str_hf_edge.py",
    "models/model/str_tar.py",
    "models/model/str_dcr.py",
    "models/model/str_reparam.py",
    "models/model/utils.py",
    "models/model/layers/vmamba_kernel.py",
