import numpy
import numpy as np
import torch
import random
import cv2


class Scale(object):
    """
    Resize the given image to a fixed scale
    """

    def __init__(self, wi, he):
        '''
        :param wi: width after resizing
        :param he: height after reszing
        '''
        self.w = wi
        self.h = he

    # modified from torchvision to add support for max size

    def __call__(self, img, label):
        '''
        :param img: RGB image
        :param label: semantic label image
        :return: resized images
        '''
        # bilinear interpolation for RGB image
        img = cv2.resize(img, (self.w, self.h))
        # nearest neighbour interpolation for label image
        label = cv2.resize(label, (self.w, self.h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
        return [img, label]


class Resize(object):
    def __init__(self, min_size, max_size, strict=False):
        if not isinstance(min_size, (list, tuple)):
            min_size = (min_size,)
        self.min_size = min_size
        self.max_size = max_size
        self.strict = strict

    # modified from torchvision to add support for max size
    def get_size(self, image_size):
        w, h = image_size
        if not self.strict:
            size = random.choice(self.min_size)
            max_size = self.max_size
            if max_size is not None:
                min_original_size = float(min((w, h)))
                max_original_size = float(max((w, h)))
                if max_original_size / min_original_size * size > max_size:
                    size = int(round(max_size * min_original_size / max_original_size))

            if (w <= h and w == size) or (h <= w and h == size):
                return (h, w)

            if w < h:
                ow = size
                oh = int(size * h / w)
            else:
                oh = size
                ow = int(size * w / h)

            return (oh, ow)
        else:
            if w < h:
                return (self.max_size, self.min_size[0])
            else:
                return (self.min_size[0], self.max_size)

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        size = self.get_size(image.shape[:2])
        image = cv2.resize(image, size)
        # I confirm that the output size is right, not reversed
        label = cv2.resize(label, size, interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
        return (image, label)


class RandomCropResize(object):
    """
    Randomly crop and resize the given image with a probability of 0.5
    """

    def __init__(self, crop_area):
        '''
        :param crop_area: area to be cropped (this is the max value and we select between 0 and crop area
        '''
        self.cw = crop_area
        self.ch = crop_area

    def __call__(self, img, label):
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            h, w = img.shape[:2]
            x1 = random.randint(0, self.ch)
            y1 = random.randint(0, self.cw)

            img_crop = img[y1:h - y1, x1:w - x1]
            label_crop = label[y1:h - y1, x1:w - x1]

            img_crop = cv2.resize(img_crop, (w, h))
            label_crop = cv2.resize(label_crop, (w, h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)

            return img_crop, label_crop
        else:
            return [img, label]


class RandomFlip(object):
    """
    Randomly flip the given Image with a probability of 0.5
    """

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        if random.random() < 0.5:
                image = cv2.flip(image, 0)  # horizontal flip
                label = cv2.flip(label, 0)  # horizontal flip
        if random.random() < 0.5:
                image = cv2.flip(image, 1)  # veritcal flip
                label = cv2.flip(label, 1)  # veritcal flip
        return [image, label]


class RandomExchange(object):
    """
    Randomly flip the given Image with a probability of 0.5
    """

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            pre_img = image[:, :, 0:3]
            post_img = image[:, :, 3:6]
            image = numpy.concatenate((post_img, pre_img), axis=2)
        return [image, label]


class Normalize(object):
    """
    Given mean: (B, G, R) and std: (B, G, R),
    will normalize each channel of the torch.*Tensor, i.e.
    channel = (channel - mean) / std
    """

    def __init__(self, mean, std):
        '''
        :param mean: global mean computed from dataset
        :param std: global std computed from dataset
        '''
        self.mean = mean
        self.std = std
        self.depth_mean = [0.5]
        self.depth_std = [0.5]

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        image = image.astype(np.float32)
        image = image / 255
        # lab contract: binarize labels with threshold gray >= 128
        # (CDD labels are JPEG with intermediate gray values)
        label = (label >= 128).astype(np.float32)
        for i in range(6):
            image[:, :, i] -= self.mean[i]
        for i in range(6):
            image[:, :, i] /= self.std[i]

        return [image, label]


class GaussianNoise(object):
    def __init__(self, std=0.05):
        '''
        :param mean: global mean computed from dataset
        :param std: global std computed from dataset
        '''
        self.std = std

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        noise = np.random.normal(loc=0, scale=self.std, size=image.shape)
        image = image + noise.astype(np.float32)
        return [image, label]


class ToTensor(object):
    '''
    This class converts the data to tensor so that it can be processed by PyTorch
    '''

    def __init__(self, scale=1):
        '''
        :param scale: set this parameter according to the output scale
        '''
        self.scale = scale

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        if self.scale != 1:
            h, w = label.shape[:2]
            image = cv2.resize(image, (int(w), int(h)))
            label = cv2.resize(label, (int(w / self.scale), int(h / self.scale)), \
                               interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
        image = image[:, :, ::-1].copy()  # .copy() is to solve "torch does not support negative index"
        image = image.transpose((2, 0, 1))
        image_tensor = torch.from_numpy(image)
        label_tensor = torch.LongTensor(np.array(label, dtype=np.int64)).unsqueeze(dim=0)

        return [image_tensor, label_tensor]


class Compose(object):
    """
    Composes several transforms together.
    """

    def __init__(self, transforms):
        self.transforms = transforms

    def __call__(self, *args):
        for t in self.transforms:
            args = t(*args)
        return args

================================================================================
# FILE: models/dataset\casa_transforms.py
================================================================================
import numpy as np
import torch
import random
import cv2
import numpy


class Scale(object):
    """Resize image and label to a fixed size"""

    def __init__(self, wi, he):
        self.w = wi
        self.h = he

    def __call__(self, img, label):
        img = cv2.resize(img, (self.w, self.h))
        label = cv2.resize(label, (self.w, self.h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
        return [img, label]


class Resize(object):
    """Resize with random short-side scaling and max-size constraint"""

    def __init__(self, min_size, max_size, strict=False):
        if not isinstance(min_size, (list, tuple)):
            min_size = (min_size,)
        self.min_size = min_size
        self.max_size = max_size
        self.strict = strict

    def get_size(self, image_size):
        w, h = image_size

        if not self.strict:
            size = random.choice(self.min_size)

            if self.max_size is not None:
                min_org = float(min(w, h))
                max_org = float(max(w, h))
                if max_org / min_org * size > self.max_size:
                    size = int(round(self.max_size * min_org / max_org))

            if (w <= h and w == size) or (h <= w and h == size):
                return (h, w)

            if w < h:
                return (int(size * h / w), size)
            else:
                return (size, int(size * w / h))
        else:
            if w < h:
                return (self.max_size, self.min_size[0])
            else:
                return (self.min_size[0], self.max_size)

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        size = self.get_size(image.shape[:2])
        image = cv2.resize(image, size)
        label = cv2.resize(label, size, interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
        return (image, label)


class RandomCropResize(object):
    """Random crop followed by resize back to original size"""

    def __init__(self, crop_area):
        self.cw = crop_area
        self.ch = crop_area

    def __call__(self, img, label):
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            h, w = img.shape[:2]
            x1 = random.randint(0, self.ch)
            y1 = random.randint(0, self.cw)

            img_crop = img[y1:h - y1, x1:w - x1]
            label_crop = label[y1:h - y1, x1:w - x1]

            img_crop = cv2.resize(img_crop, (w, h))
            label_crop = cv2.resize(label_crop, (w, h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
            return img_crop, label_crop

        return [img, label]


class RandomFlip(object):
    """Random horizontal and vertical flip"""

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            image = cv2.flip(image, 0)
            label = cv2.flip(label, 0)
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            image = cv2.flip(image, 1)
            label = cv2.flip(label, 1)
        return [image, label]


class RandomExchange(object):
    """Randomly swap pre- and post-event images (for bi-temporal input)"""

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            pre_img = image[:, :, 0:3]
            post_img = image[:, :, 3:6]
            image = numpy.concatenate((post_img, pre_img), axis=2)
        return [image, label]


class Normalize(object):
    """Normalize image using dataset mean and std"""

    def __init__(self, mean, std):
        self.mean = mean
        self.std = std

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        image = image.astype(np.float32) / 255.0
        label = (label >= 128).astype(np.float32)

        for i in range(6):
            image[:, :, i] = (image[:, :, i] - self.mean[i]) / self.std[i]

        return [image, label]


class GaussianNoise(object):
    """Add Gaussian noise to image"""

    def __init__(self, std=0.05):
        self.std = std

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        noise = np.random.normal(0, self.std, size=image.shape)
        image = image + noise.astype(np.float32)
        return [image, label]


class ToTensor(object):
    """Convert numpy arrays to PyTorch tensors"""

    def __init__(self, scale=1):
        self.scale = scale

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        if self.scale != 1:
            h, w = label.shape[:2]
            image = cv2.resize(image, (w, h))
            label = cv2.resize(
                label,
                (int(w / self.scale), int(h / self.scale)),
                interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST
            )

        image = image[:, :, ::-1].copy()      # BGR → RGB
        image = image.transpose((2, 0, 1))    # HWC → CHW

        image_tensor = torch.from_numpy(image)
        label_tensor = torch.LongTensor(
            np.array(label, dtype=np.int_)
        ).unsqueeze(0)

        return [image_tensor, label_tensor]


class Compose(object):
    """Compose multiple transforms"""

    def __init__(self, transforms):
        self.transforms = transforms

    def __call__(self, *args):
        for t in self.transforms:
            args = t(*args)
        return args

# FILE: models/dataset\dataset.py
================================================================================
import cv2
import os
from os.path import join as osp
import numpy
import torch.utils.data


def read_list(list_path):
    """Read a list/*.txt file: one sample name per line (no extension).

    The server-side dataset layout is `<root>/{A,B,label}/` + `<root>/list/*.txt`.
    """
    with open(list_path, "r") as f:
        names = [line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()]
    return names


class Dataset(torch.utils.data.Dataset):
    """Bi-temporal change detection dataset in A/B/label + list format.

    Args:
        file_root: dataset root containing A/ B/ label/ dirs
        list_path: path to list/*.txt with sample names (no extension)
        transform: Compose of Transforms
    """
    def __init__(self, file_root='data/', list_path=None, mode='train', transform=None):
        if list_path is not None and os.path.isfile(list_path):
            self.file_list = read_list(list_path)
        else:
            # legacy mode: file_root/<mode>/{A,B,label} with natural order
            self.file_list = os.listdir(osp(file_root, mode, 'A'))
            file_root = osp(file_root, mode)

        self.pre_images = [osp(file_root, 'A', x) for x in self.file_list]
        self.post_images = [osp(file_root, 'B', x) for x in self.file_list]
        self.gts = [osp(file_root, 'label', x) for x in self.file_list]

        self.transform = transform

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.pre_images)

    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        pre_image_name = self.pre_images[idx]
        label_name = self.gts[idx]
        post_image_name = self.post_images[idx]

        pre_image = cv2.imread(pre_image_name)
        label = cv2.imread(label_name, 0)
        post_image = cv2.imread(post_image_name)

        img = numpy.concatenate((pre_image, post_image), axis=2)

        if self.transform:
            [img, label] = self.transform(img, label)

        return img, label

    def get_img_info(self, idx):
        img = cv2.imread(self.pre_images[idx])
        return {"height": img.shape[0], "width": img.shape[1]}

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\b4_spe_head.py
================================================================================
"""Run8 B4-SPE head：B4 Semantic Pair Evidence + Sub-patch Expansion。

设计（CASA-CD_Run8_B4-SPE最终路线与预注册.md §5）：
  输入 fx=[Vx]、fy=[Vy]，V = frozen ViT4 final-LN B4 token（B×256×192）。
  单一 Symmetric Pair Descriptor（严格时间交换对称）：

      D = [ |T_A − T_B| , (T_A + T_B)/2 ]     # 384
      Z = Linear(384 → 96, bias)              # B×256×96 → 96×16×16

  然后 Sub-patch Expansion（复用 Run7 已 smoke 的四级拓扑，无 attention/gate/残差）：

      96×16×16 → Conv1×1 96→128 + BN + ReLU → PixelShuffle×2 → 32×32×32 → DW3×3+BN+ReLU
              → Conv1×1 32→64  → PS×2 → 64×64×16 → DW3×3
              → Conv1×1 16→64  → PS×2 → 128×128×16 → DW3×3
              → Conv1×1 16→16  → PS×2 → 256×256×4 → DW3×3 → Conv1×1 4→1 → sigmoid

参数：pair 36,960 + expansion 16,912 = **53,872**（head 全部 trainable）。
整体 = ViT4 1,976,832 + head 53,872 = **2,030,704**。
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class B4SPEHead(nn.Module):
    """B4-SPE：Change-Sensitive B4 Semantic Source + Symmetric Pair Descriptor + Sub-patch Expansion。"""

    def __init__(self, token_dim=192, proj_dim=96):
        super().__init__()
        self.pair = nn.Linear(token_dim * 2, proj_dim, bias=True)

        self.exp1 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(96, 128, 1, bias=False), nn.BatchNorm2d(128), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.dw1 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(32, 32, 3, 1, padding=1, groups=32, bias=False),
                                 nn.BatchNorm2d(32), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.exp2 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 1, bias=False), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.dw2 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(16, 16, 3, 1, padding=1, groups=16, bias=False),
                                 nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.exp3 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(16, 64, 1, bias=False), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.dw3 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(16, 16, 3, 1, padding=1, groups=16, bias=False),
                                 nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.exp4 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(16, 16, 1, bias=False), nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.dw4 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(4, 4, 3, 1, padding=1, groups=4, bias=False),
                                 nn.BatchNorm2d(4), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.out = nn.Conv2d(4, 1, 1, bias=False)

    def forward(self, fx, fy):
        vx = fx[0]                                    # (B,192,16,16)
        vy = fy[0]
        B, C, H, W = vx.shape
        tx = vx.flatten(2).transpose(1, 2)            # (B,256,192)
        ty = vy.flatten(2).transpose(1, 2)
        d = torch.cat([torch.abs(tx - ty), (tx + ty) / 2.0], dim=-1)   # (B,256,384)
        z = self.pair(d)                              # (B,256,96)
        x = z.transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, 96, 16, 16)

        x = self.dw1(F.pixel_shuffle(self.exp1(x), 2))      # 32×32×32
        x = self.dw2(F.pixel_shuffle(self.exp2(x), 2))      # 64×64×16
        x = self.dw3(F.pixel_shuffle(self.exp3(x), 2))      # 128×128×16
        x = self.dw4(F.pixel_shuffle(self.exp4(x), 2))      # 256×256×4
        return torch.sigmoid(self.out(x))

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\casa_net.py
================================================================================
"""CASA: MobileNetV2/HFEA with independent context modules, TAR and DCR."""
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F

from model.mobilenet_v2 import mobilenet_v2
from model.encoder_fusion import EncoderFusion
from model.str_tar import MultiScaleTAR
from model.str_dcr import DCRDecoder
from model.layers.change_context_qkv import ChangeAwareCompressedQKV
from model.layers.hyperbolic_context_qkv import HyperbolicCompressedQKV


class CASANet(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, pretrained_path=None, module="none"):
        super().__init__()
        if module not in ("none", "hyperbolic", "qkv"):
            raise ValueError(module)
        self.module = module
        self.encoder = mobilenet_v2(pretrained_path)
        self.encoder_fusion = EncoderFusion([16, 24, 32, 96, 320], midc=32, outc=64)
        self.tar = MultiScaleTAR((64, 64, 64, 64), dim=64,
                                 use_temporal_aux=True, use_dcr_aux=True,
                                 use_residual=True)
        self.decoder = DCRDecoder(dim=64, use_aux=True, use_residual=True)
        self.head = nn.Conv2d(64, 1, 1)
        # Construct after the common path so all variants share its initialization.
        with torch.random.fork_rng():
            torch.manual_seed(2333)
            block = {"hyperbolic": HyperbolicCompressedQKV,
                     "qkv": ChangeAwareCompressedQKV}.get(module)
            self.stage_modules = nn.ModuleList([block(64, 32) for _ in range(4)] if block else [])

    def forward(self, a, b):
        features_a = self.encoder_fusion(*self.encoder(a))
        features_b = self.encoder_fusion(*self.encoder(b))
        if self.stage_modules:
            pairs = [op(x, y) for op, x, y in zip(self.stage_modules, features_a, features_b)]
            features_a, features_b = zip(*pairs)
        fused = self.tar(features_a, features_b)
        pyramid = self.decoder.forward_pyramid(fused)
        return tuple(torch.sigmoid(F.interpolate(self.head(x), size=a.shape[-2:],
                                                mode="bilinear", align_corners=False))
                     for x in pyramid)

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        self.tar.switch_to_deploy()
        self.decoder.switch_to_deploy()
        return self

    def diagnostics(self):
        return {f"stage{i + 1}": dict(op.last_diag) for i, op in enumerate(self.stage_modules)}

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\casa_tvim_str_net.py
================================================================================
"""CASA-TViM-STRNet：CAACP-SS2D 主线架构（TinyViM-S-Slim + CAACP + TAR/DCR）。

数据流（调研文档 §10，2B 拼接 siamese，BN 共享 batch 统计）：
    [A;B] (2B) -> TinyViM-S-Slim shared
        stem -> F1(1/4,48) -> F2(1/8,64) -> stage2 prefix(1/16,168)
        -> [CAACP 共享 score] -> stage2 final TViM -> F3(1/16,168)
        -> slim stage4 -> F4(1/32,224)
    per-time [F1,F2,F3,F4] -> MultiScaleTAR(encoder_dims=(48,64,168,224), D=96)
        -> DCRDecoder(D=96) -> head Conv1x1 -> bilinear -> sigmoid

变体（caacp × score_mode × residual_mode × frh × fs_tar × rep_mode，共享同一实现）：
    A0_TVIM_PLAIN: caacp=0, plain    A1_CAACP: caacp=1, plain
    A2_STR:        caacp=0, full     M1_FULL:  caacp=1, full
    Run2 E1_CP_CAACP: caacp=1, score_mode=cp, frh=0
    Run2 E2_FRH:     caacp=1, score_mode=rank, frh=1
    Run2 E3_CP_FRH:  caacp=1, score_mode=cp, frh=1
    Run3 E4_RA_CAACP: caacp=1, residual_mode=avg_anchor（residual 锚定 c_avg）
    Run3 E5_FS_TAR:   caacp=1, fs_tar=1（stage1 TemporalRepFine3x3）
    Run4 E6_FET1:     caacp=1, fine_tap=1（1/4 细尺度证据 1×1 可折叠旁路 → DCR refine 之后相加）

RNG 纪律：head 先于 TAR/DCR aux 构造；CAACP 只有 zero-init β（无 RNG 消耗）；
FRH gamma zero-init（无 RNG），dw3 kaiming / aux skip_init；
FS-TAR 的 aux 分支 skip_init + zero-init（与 TemporalRep1x1 同流）；
FET1 **最后构造**且用 fork_rng + 固定局部种子（不消耗全局随机流，保证开关 fine_tap 时
既有模块初始化逐位相同）。
deploy：switch_to_deploy() 折叠 TAR/DCR/FRH/FET1；CAACP 是推理期真实模块。
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from model.tinyvim_s_slim import TinyViMSlim
from model.layers.caacp_ss2d import change_score_cosine_2d, rank_normalize_2d
from model.str_tar import MultiScaleTAR
from model.str_dcr import DCRDecoder
from model.str_fine_head import STRFineHead
from model.str_fine_tap import FineEvidenceTap1x1, FET_FORM, FET_FUSE, FET_SOURCE
from model.str_hf_edge import (HF_EDGE_FORM, HF_EDGE_FORMS, HF_EDGE_LAMBDA_DEFAULT,
                               HF_EDGE_TAP_KEYS, HF_EDGE_TAPS, build_hf_edge_head)

ENCODER_DIMS = (48, 64, 168, 224)
STR_DIM = 96


class CASATViMSTRNet(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, tinyvim_pretrained_path, caacp=True, rep_mode="full",
                 str_dim=STR_DIM, caacp_score_mode="rank", frh=False,
                 caacp_residual_mode="current", fs_tar=False, fine_tap=False,
                 hf_edge=False, hf_edge_form="none", hf_edge_taps="1_4_1_8",
                 hf_edge_lambda=HF_EDGE_LAMBDA_DEFAULT):
        super().__init__()
        assert rep_mode in ("plain", "full")
        assert caacp_score_mode in ("rank", "cp")
        assert caacp_residual_mode in ("current", "avg_anchor")
        self.caacp = caacp
        self.rep_mode = rep_mode
        self.str_dim = str_dim
        self.caacp_score_mode = caacp_score_mode
        self.caacp_residual_mode = caacp_residual_mode
        self.frh = frh
        self.fs_tar = fs_tar
        self.fine_tap = bool(fine_tap)

        # ---- Run7 HFAE（fail-closed：开关与形态必须自洽）
        self.hf_edge = bool(hf_edge)
        if self.hf_edge:
            assert hf_edge_form in ("aux_only", "aux_inject"), \
                f"[ARCH-MISMATCH] hf_edge=1 requires hf_edge_form in aux_only|aux_inject, got {hf_edge_form}"
            assert not self.fine_tap, "[ARCH-MISMATCH] hf_edge and fine_tap are mutually exclusive"
            assert hf_edge_taps in HF_EDGE_TAPS, f"unknown hf_edge_taps {hf_edge_taps}"
        else:
            assert hf_edge_form in ("none", None), \
                f"[ARCH-MISMATCH] hf_edge=0 requires hf_edge_form=none, got {hf_edge_form}"
        self.hf_edge_form = hf_edge_form if self.hf_edge else "none"
        self.hf_edge_taps = hf_edge_taps if self.hf_edge else "none"
        self.hf_edge_lambda = float(hf_edge_lambda)
        self.hf_edge_form_tag = HF_EDGE_FORM if self.hf_edge else None
        self._hf_taps = {}
        self._hf_handles = []
        self._hf_edge_logits = None
        self._hf_edge_feat = None

        # R4 sidecar 标识（fine_tap=0 时为 None，保持旧行为逐位不变）
        self.fine_tap_form = FET_FORM if self.fine_tap else None
        self.fine_tap_source = FET_SOURCE if self.fine_tap else None
        self.fine_tap_fuse = FET_FUSE if self.fine_tap else None

        self.encoder = TinyViMSlim(pretrained_path=tinyvim_pretrained_path, caacp=caacp,
                                   caacp_score_mode=caacp_score_mode,
                                   caacp_residual_mode=caacp_residual_mode)
        if self.encoder.caacp_op is not None:
            assert self.encoder.caacp_op.score_mode == caacp_score_mode
            assert self.encoder.caacp_op.residual_mode == caacp_residual_mode

        # head 先构造（STR RNG 纪律）；CAACP 已在 encoder 内（β zero-init，无 RNG）
        if frh:
            self.head = STRFineHead(str_dim)
        else:
            self.head = nn.Conv2d(str_dim, 1, 1)
        self.tar = MultiScaleTAR(
            encoder_dims=ENCODER_DIMS, dim=str_dim,
            use_temporal_aux=(rep_mode == "full"),
            use_dcr_aux=(rep_mode == "full"), use_residual=True,
            fine_stage1=fs_tar,
        )
        self.decoder = DCRDecoder(dim=str_dim, use_aux=(rep_mode == "full"), use_residual=True)

        # R4-FET1：**最后构造**，局部 RNG 初始化（不推进全局随机流）
        if self.fine_tap:
            assert str_dim == 96 and ENCODER_DIMS[0] == 48, "FET1 假设 C=48(1/4) → D=96"
            self.fine_evidence_tap = FineEvidenceTap1x1(
                in_ch=ENCODER_DIMS[0], out_ch=str_dim, deploy=False)
        else:
            self.fine_evidence_tap = None

        # R7-HFAE：同样**最后构造**；HFEdgeHead 内部用 fork_rng + 固定局部种子，
        # 不推进全局随机流 ⇒ 开关 hf_edge 时既有模块初始化逐位相同（T2 验证）。
        if self.hf_edge:
            assert str_dim == 96, "HFAE 假设 D=96（注入维度）"
            self.hf_edge_head = build_hf_edge_head(
                taps=self.hf_edge_taps, form=self.hf_edge_form, inject_dim=str_dim)
            self._register_hf_hooks()
        else:
            self.hf_edge_head = None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ R7 HFAE
    def _hf_hook_targets(self):
        return [self.encoder.network[n][b].op.local_conv
                for (n, b, _c, _r) in HF_EDGE_TAPS[self.hf_edge_taps]]

    def _ensure_hf_hooks(self):
        """幂等地确保 tap hook 挂在**当前**模块上。

        存在的理由：`copy.deepcopy(model)` 会复制 nn.Module 树，但**不会**复制挂在原模块上的
        forward hook —— 而项目的 D0/eval 审计路径正是 `deepcopy` + `switch_to_deploy`。
        因此每次 forward 前做一次轻量检查：当前模块上若没有我们的 hook 就补挂。

        R7 hotfix（2026-10-11，详见预注册 §7.7）：标记必须打在 **hook 函数**上——
        `nn.Module._forward_hooks` 的 value 是 hook 函数本身，而 `register_forward_hook()`
        返回的 `RemovableHandle` 是**另一个对象**、并不存放于该 dict。此前误把标记打在 handle 上，
        于是这里的 `attached` 恒为 False，**每次 forward 都重复注册 1 个 hook/模块**：hook 数随
        forward 数无界增长（含每 epoch 的全测试集验证 ⇒ 80K 步约 20 万个），单步耗时按
        O(累计 forward) 线性上升（实测每 hook ≈ 2.2 μs/forward），波二四库因此劣化约 4×。
        该缺陷只影响 `hf_edge=1` 的 run（`hf_edge=0` 在下方立即 return ⇒ CTRL 不受影响）。

        每个 handle 与目标模块成对保存，`switch_to_deploy()` 只移除属于**本模型**的 hook，
        避免 deepcopy 的副本误删原模型上的 hook。
        """
        if not self.hf_edge or self.hf_edge_head is None:
            return
        keys = HF_EDGE_TAP_KEYS[self.hf_edge_taps]
        for key, mod in zip(keys, self._hf_hook_targets()):
            mine = [fn for fn in getattr(mod, "_forward_hooks", {}).values()
                    if getattr(fn, "_hfae_key", None) == key]
            # fail-closed 不变式：同一 tap 模块上属于本 key 的 hook 至多 1 个。
            # 若幂等检查再被写坏，这里在第 2 次 forward 就抛错，而不是静默把训练拖慢 N 倍
            # （该缺陷曾静默运行 7 小时 / 21 万步，见预注册 §7.7）。
            # 该判据对 deepcopy 路径安全：副本的模块上不带任何我们的 hook。
            if len(mine) > 1:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"[HFAE] hook 幂等性被破坏：tap {key} 上挂了 {len(mine)} 个 hook"
                    "（应恰好 1 个；见预注册 §7.7）")
            if mine:
                continue
            fn = (lambda k: (lambda m, i, o: self._hf_taps.__setitem__(k, o)))(key)
            fn._hfae_key = key
            self._hf_handles.append((mod.register_forward_hook(fn), mod))

    def _remove_hf_hooks(self):
        """只移除仍然挂在本模型模块上的 hook（deepcopy 副本不会误删原模型的）。"""
        for h, mod in self._hf_handles:
            try:
                if getattr(h, "id", None) in getattr(mod, "_forward_hooks", {}):
                    h.remove()
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
                pass
        self._hf_handles = []

    def _register_hf_hooks(self):
        """构造期注册（后续由 `_ensure_hf_hooks()` 保证幂等）。"""
        self._hf_handles = []
        self._ensure_hf_hooks()

    def hf_edge_hook_counts(self):
        """诊断（T12 回归测试用）：每个 tap 模块上属于本模型的 hook 数，应恒为 1。

        R7 hotfix §7.7：该值曾随 forward 次数无界增长（每次 forward +1/模块），
        是波二 4× 减速的直接原因；本访问器让"幂等性"成为可断言、可回归的量。
        """
        if not self.hf_edge:
            return {}
        keys = HF_EDGE_TAP_KEYS[self.hf_edge_taps]
        return {key: sum(1 for fn in getattr(mod, "_forward_hooks", {}).values()
                         if getattr(fn, "_hfae_key", None) == key)
                for key, mod in zip(keys, self._hf_hook_targets())}

    def _run_hf_edge(self):
        """从 hook 缓存取高频 tap → 边缘头。

        返回 `(edge_logits (B,1,h,w), edge_feat (B,mid,h,w))`——双时相在头内**配对**，
        因此是 B 尺寸（每对样品一张变化边缘图）。
        """
        keys = HF_EDGE_TAP_KEYS[self.hf_edge_taps]
        taps = []
        for k in keys:
            t = self._hf_taps.get(k)
            if t is None:
                raise RuntimeError(f"[HFAE] tap {k} missing: forward hooks did not fire")
            taps.append(t)
        logits, feat = self.hf_edge_head(taps)
        self._hf_taps.clear()                       # 释放对 autograd 图的引用
        return logits, feat

    def aux_edge_logits(self):
        """训练期边缘 logits（`train.py` 用它算辅助损失；步间不保留旧值）。"""
        return self._hf_edge_logits

    # ---- R7 HFAE 机制自检：辅助损失是否真的回流到「高频分支」
    def _hf_tap_modules(self):
        if not self.hf_edge:
            return []
        return [self.encoder.network[n][b].op.local_conv
                for (n, b, _c, _r) in HF_EDGE_TAPS[self.hf_edge_taps]]

    def hf_edge_tap_grad_norm(self):
        """被 tap 的高频分支（`local_conv`）上辅助损失的梯度 L2 范数。

        > 0 才说明边缘监督在**重塑表征**；若恒为 0，则本机制退化为"旁路头"，
        与已被否证的无监督注入等价 ⇒ T5 的判定依据。
        """
        tot = 0.0
        for mod in self._hf_tap_modules():
            for p in mod.parameters():
                if p.grad is not None:
                    tot += float(p.grad.detach().pow(2).sum())
        return tot ** 0.5

    def hf_edge_tap_grad_nonzero(self):
        n = 0
        for mod in self._hf_tap_modules():
            for p in mod.parameters():
                if p.grad is not None and float(p.grad.detach().abs().sum()) > 0.0:
                    n += 1
        return n

    def hf_edge_head_grad_norm(self):
        if self.hf_edge_head is None:
            return 0.0
        tot = sum(float(p.grad.detach().pow(2).sum())
                  for p in self.hf_edge_head.parameters() if p.grad is not None)
        return tot ** 0.5

    def hf_edge_report(self):
        if not self.hf_edge or self.hf_edge_head is None:
            return None
        rep = self.hf_edge_head.param_report()
        rep.update({"form": self.hf_edge_form, "taps": self.hf_edge_taps,
                    "lambda": self.hf_edge_lambda, "tag": self.hf_edge_form_tag,
                    "deploy_dropped": bool(self.hf_edge_form == "aux_only"
                                           and not self._hf_handles
                                           and self.hf_edge_head is None)})
        return rep

    def forward(self, pre, post, label=None):
        if self.hf_edge and self.hf_edge_head is not None:
            self._ensure_hf_hooks()                             # deepcopy 后自动补挂
            self._hf_taps.clear()                               # 步间不持有旧图
            self._hf_edge_logits = None
            self._hf_edge_feat = None
        x2b = torch.cat([pre, post], dim=0)                     # (2B,3,256,256)
        f1, f2, x = self.encoder.forward_stem_s2_prefix(x2b)    # ← tap 在此处被 hook 捕获

        if self.hf_edge and self.hf_edge_head is not None:
            self._hf_edge_logits, self._hf_edge_feat = self._run_hf_edge()

        if self.caacp and self.encoder.caacp_op is not None:
            xa, xb = x.chunk(2, dim=0)
            with torch.no_grad():
                s_abs = change_score_cosine_2d(xa, xb)          # (B,H,W) in [0,2]
                s_rank = rank_normalize_2d(s_abs)
                s_rank2b = torch.cat([s_rank, s_rank], dim=0)   # A/B 共享权重
                s_abs2b = (torch.cat([s_abs, s_abs], dim=0)
                           if self.caacp_score_mode == "cp" else None)
            self.encoder.caacp_op.set_pair_score(s_rank2b, abs_score=s_abs2b)
        x = self.encoder.forward_caacp_block(x)
        f3 = self.encoder.norm4(x)
        f4 = self.encoder.forward_stage4(x)

        f1a, f1b = f1.chunk(2, dim=0)
        f2a, f2b = f2.chunk(2, dim=0)
        f3a, f3b = f3.chunk(2, dim=0)
        f4a, f4b = f4.chunk(2, dim=0)

        feats = self.tar([f1a, f2a, f3a, f4a], [f1b, f2b, f3b, f4b])
        if (self.hf_edge and self.hf_edge_form == "aux_inject"
                and self._hf_edge_feat is not None):
            # 零初始化注入 ⇒ epoch-0 逐位恒等；注入到 DCR 的最小尺度输入（1/4）
            assert self.hf_edge_head.inject is not None, "[HFAE] aux_inject requires inject proj"
            feats = list(feats)
            feats[0] = feats[0] + self.hf_edge_head.inject(self._hf_edge_feat)
        x = self.decoder(feats)                     # 原始 DCR.refine 输出 [B,96,64,64]
        if self.fine_tap:
            # R4-FET1：refine **之后**相加（不进入 SiLU/RepLocalBlock，保持 DCR 训练路径不变）
            x = x + self.fine_evidence_tap(f1a, f1b)
        logits = self.head(x)       # frh: 内部 64² -> 128²；plain: 64²
        logits = F.interpolate(logits, size=pre.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
        return torch.sigmoid(logits)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        self.tar.switch_to_deploy()
        self.decoder.switch_to_deploy()
        if self.frh:
            self.head.switch_to_deploy()
        if self.fine_tap:
            self.fine_evidence_tap.switch_to_deploy()
        if self.hf_edge and self.hf_edge_head is not None:
            self._remove_hf_hooks()
            if self.hf_edge_form == "aux_only":
                # 训练期深监督 ⇒ 推理图不含边缘头，deploy 参数与基线**完全相同**
                self.hf_edge_head = None
            else:
                self.hf_edge_head.drop_supervision_head()   # 只丢监督专用 edge_out
            self._hf_taps.clear()
            self._hf_edge_logits = None
            self._hf_edge_feat = None
        return self

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ R4 diag
    def fet_gamma(self):
        if self.fine_tap and self.fine_evidence_tap is not None and not self.fine_evidence_tap.deploy:
            return float(self.fine_evidence_tap.gamma.detach().cpu().item())
        return None

    def fet_deploy_conv_shape(self):
        if self.fine_tap and self.fine_evidence_tap is not None and self.fine_evidence_tap.deploy:
            w = self.fine_evidence_tap.fused.weight
            return [int(w.shape[0]), int(w.shape[1]), int(w.shape[2]), int(w.shape[3])]
        return None


    def caacp_beta(self):
        if self.encoder.caacp_op is not None:
            return float(self.encoder.caacp_op.beta.detach().cpu().item())
        return None

    def caacp_score_delta(self):
        if self.encoder.caacp_op is not None:
            return float(self.encoder.caacp_op._score_delta)
        return None

    def caacp_abs_mean(self):
        if self.encoder.caacp_op is not None:
            return self.encoder.caacp_op.abs_mean()
        return None

    def caacp_weight_entropy(self):
        if self.encoder.caacp_op is not None:
            return self.encoder.caacp_op.weight_entropy()
        return None

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\casa_vmamba_net.py
================================================================================
"""CASA-VMamba-STRNet：Run8 主线（VMamba-Tiny 骨干 + CAACP-HQKV + TAR/DCR）。

## 数据流（真实，对应 `forward()` 逐行）

```
T1 [B,3,256,256] ─┐
                   ├─ concat dim0 ─ VMamba-Tiny (2B) ──► f1(1/4,96) f2(1/8,192) f3(1/16,384) f4(1/32,768)
T2 [B,3,256,256] ─┘                                        │
                                                           ├─ chunk(2) → 每时相各 4 个 stage
                                                           │
                    CAACP-HQKV（Run8 创新）◄────────────────┘
                      · 每 stage 双曲映射 → detail / global 两分支
                      · 每分支特化 QKV，K/V 压缩到 k·N，Q 保持 N
                      · 4 个 stage 的增量上采样到 1/4 相加 → γ 零初始化门
                                                           │
                    feats[0] += γ·Δ        （R4：注入**跳层连接**/解码器最小尺度输入）
                                                           │
                       MultiScaleTAR(192,192,192,192, D) ──► DCRDecoder(D) ──► head Conv1x1
                                                           │
                                            bilinear 到 256² ──► sigmoid
```

## 变体开关（唯一变量纪律）

| 开关 | 含义 |
|---|---|
| `hqkv` | 0=不加 CAACP-HQKV（同期 CTRL）；1=加 |
| `geometry` | `hyperbolic`（双曲，主实验）/ `euclidean`（**同式欧氏对照**，调研强制要求） |
| `branch` | `both` / `detail_only` / `global_only`（分支特化的消融） |
| `hgsl` | 0=无新损失；1=HGSL 开启；`hgsl_detach_score` 决定梯度是否进入几何量 |
| `inject_mode` | `dcr1`（只注 1/4）/ `dcr1_16`（1/4 与 1/16 两处跳层）/ `none` |
| `hf_edge` | Run8 **不默认启用**（VMamba 没有 TinyViM 的 `local_conv` 高频旁路，HFAE 的 tap 不存在）；保留开关仅为兼容性检查 |

## 预训练纪律

1. 主干 `VMamba-Tiny` 的 222 个 key 全部逐位加载（`worst_diff` 必须为 **0**，见 `vmamba_tiny.load_pretrained`）；
2. CAACP-HQKV **最后构造** + `fork_rng` 固定局部种子 ⇒ 不推进全局随机流；
3. 三个门 `γ` **零初始化**（在 `apply` 之后）⇒ `hqkv=1` 与 `hqkv=0` 在 **epoch-0 逐位一致**
   （由 T0 smoke 用真实 batch 断言）；
4. TAR/DCR 的辅助分支保持 Run1–Run7 的零初始化约定 ⇒ `switch_to_deploy()` 折叠门仍然有效。
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from model.layers.caacp_hqkv import HQKV_FORM, HQKV_SEED, build_caacp_hqkv
from model.str_dcr import DCRDecoder
from model.str_tar import MultiScaleTAR
from model.vmamba_tiny import STAGE_RES, VMambaBackbone, build_vmamba_tiny

ENCODER_DIMS = (96, 192, 384, 768)
STR_DIM = 192
GEOMETRIES = ("hyperbolic", "euclidean")
BRANCHES = ("both", "detail_only", "global_only")
INJECT_MODES = ("none", "dcr1", "dcr1_16")


class CASAVMambaSTRNet(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, vmamba_pretrained_path=None, hqkv=True, str_dim=STR_DIM,
                 geometry="hyperbolic", branch="both", hgsl=True,
                 hgsl_detach_score=True, inject_mode="dcr1",
                 hqkv_rank=32, hqkv_lite_dim=192,
                 hqkv_detail_window=2, hqkv_global_window=4,
                 hqkv_attn_stages=(1, 2, 3),
                 rep_mode="full", fs_tar=False,
                 vmamba_cfg=None, hf_edge=False, hf_edge_form="none",
                 hqkv_r0=0.5, hqkv_a=0.5, hqkv_seed=HQKV_SEED):
        super().__init__()
        assert geometry in GEOMETRIES, geometry
        assert branch in BRANCHES, branch
        assert inject_mode in INJECT_MODES, inject_mode
        assert rep_mode in ("plain", "full")
        assert not hf_edge, (
            "[ARCH] Run8 不启用 HFAE：VMamba 没有 TinyViM 的 `local_conv` 高频旁路，"
            "HF_EDGE_TAPS 指向的模块不存在。若确需高频损失，请改用 HGSL（已内置）。")

        self.hqkv = bool(hqkv)
        self.geometry = geometry
        self.branch = branch
        self.hgsl = bool(hgsl)
        self.hgsl_detach_score = bool(hgsl_detach_score)
        self.inject_mode = inject_mode
        self.rep_mode = rep_mode
        self.str_dim = int(str_dim)
        self.fs_tar = bool(fs_tar)
        self.hqkv_form = HQKV_FORM if self.hqkv else None
        self.hf_edge = False

        # ---- 主干（VMamba-Tiny，官方 v2 权重）
        self.encoder = build_vmamba_tiny(pretrained_path=vmamba_pretrained_path,
                                         cfg=vmamba_cfg)

        # ---- head / TAR / DCR（与 Run1–Run7 同族，输入维改为 VMamba 的 (96..768)）
        self.head = nn.Conv2d(self.str_dim, 1, 1)
        self.tar = MultiScaleTAR(
            encoder_dims=ENCODER_DIMS, dim=self.str_dim,
            use_temporal_aux=(rep_mode == "full"),
            use_dcr_aux=(rep_mode == "full"), use_residual=True,
            fine_stage1=self.fs_tar,
        )
        self.decoder = DCRDecoder(dim=self.str_dim, use_aux=(rep_mode == "full"),
                                  use_residual=True)

        # ---- CAACP-HQKV（**最后构造** + fork_rng）
        self._scores = {}
        self._diag = {}
        if self.hqkv:
            with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):
                torch.manual_seed(int(hqkv_seed))
                self.hqkv_op = build_caacp_hqkv(
                    stage_dims=ENCODER_DIMS, rank=hqkv_rank,
                    detail_window=hqkv_detail_window, global_window=hqkv_global_window,
                    attn_stages=hqkv_attn_stages, lite_dim=hqkv_lite_dim,
                    r0=hqkv_r0, a=hqkv_a, init_seed=int(hqkv_seed),
                    score_stages=(0, 1, 2, 3) if (self.hgsl and not self.hgsl_detach_score)
                    else ())
                # γ 零初始化（在 apply 之后，见 HQKV 内注释）
                nn.init.zeros_(self.hqkv_op.gamma_detail)
                nn.init.zeros_(self.hqkv_op.gamma_global)
                nn.init.zeros_(self.hqkv_op.gamma_lite)
        else:
            self.hqkv_op = None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ 诊断
    def hqkv_report(self):
        if self.hqkv_op is None:
            return None
        rep = self.hqkv_op.param_report()
        rep.update({"geometry": self.geometry, "branch": self.branch,
                    "inject_mode": self.inject_mode,
                    "hgsl": self.hgsl, "hgsl_detach_score": self.hgsl_detach_score})
        return rep

    def hgsl_score_maps(self):
        """供 `hgsl_loss` 使用：每 stage 的双曲变化分数（B,1,H_i,W_i）。"""
        if self.hqkv_op is None:
            return {}
        return self.hqkv_op.score_maps()

    def hqkv_diag(self):
        return dict(getattr(self.hqkv_op, "_diag", {}) if self.hqkv_op is not None else {})

    def pretrain_load_stats(self):
        return self.encoder.load_stats()

    def param_breakdown(self):
        enc = sum(p.numel() for p in self.encoder.parameters())
        tar = sum(p.numel() for p in self.tar.parameters())
        dcr = sum(p.numel() for p in self.decoder.parameters())
        head = sum(p.numel() for p in self.head.parameters())
        hq = (sum(p.numel() for p in self.hqkv_op.parameters())
              if self.hqkv_op is not None else 0)
        total = sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters())
        return {"backbone": enc, "hqkv": hq, "tar": tar, "dcr": dcr, "head": head,
                "total": total, "non_backbone": total - enc,
                "non_backbone_no_hqkv": total - enc - hq}

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(self, pre, post, label=None):
        x2b = torch.cat([pre, post], dim=0)                  # (2B,3,256,256)
        outs = self.encoder.forward_features(x2b)            # 4 × (2B, C_i, H_i, W_i)
        del x2b

        # 每时相各自的 4 个 stage（**不是** A/B 拼接后再分）
        feats_a, feats_b = [], []
        for t in outs:
            a, b = t.chunk(2, dim=0)
            feats_a.append(a)
            feats_b.append(b)
        del outs

        if self.hqkv_op is not None:
            out, diag = self.hqkv_op(feats_a, feats_b, geometry=self.geometry)
            self._diag = diag
            self._scores = self.hqkv_op.score_maps()
            # R4：注入到 DCR 的最小尺度输入（1/4）。`dcr1_16` 额外在 1/16 处注入。
            if self.inject_mode in ("dcr1", "dcr1_16"):
                delta = out
                if self.inject_mode == "dcr1_16" and self.str_dim == delta.shape[1]:
                    feats_a[1] = feats_a[1] + F.interpolate(
                        delta, size=feats_a[1].shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
            else:
                delta = None
        else:
            delta = None
            self._diag = {}
            self._scores = {}

        feats = self.tar(feats_a, feats_b)                   # 4 × (B, D, H_i, W_i)
        if delta is not None:
            assert feats[0].shape[-2:] == delta.shape[-2:], \
                f"[INJECT] 尺度不匹配 feats[0]={tuple(feats[0].shape[-2:])} delta={tuple(delta.shape[-2:])}"
            feats = list(feats)
            feats[0] = feats[0] + delta
        x = self.decoder(feats)
        logits = self.head(x)
        logits = F.interpolate(logits, size=pre.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
        return torch.sigmoid(logits)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        """把 TAR/DCR 的训练多分支折叠为单卷积。

        **CAACP-HQKV 保留在部署图里**，理由（必须写进论文，不能含糊）：
        它是**推理期真实执行的算子**（和 Run1–Run7 的 CAACP 一样），而且
        `train↔deploy` 折叠门要求两条图的前向**逐位一致**——若部署时把 HQKV 删掉，
        `feats[0]` 就会少一项，折叠门必然失败。
        ⇒ Run8 的"轻量"主张只能来自 **HQKV 自身的参数很小**（见 `param_breakdown()`），
        不能声称它零推理代价。
        """
        self.tar.switch_to_deploy()
        self.decoder.switch_to_deploy()
        return self

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\casa_vmamba_net_v2.py
================================================================================
"""CASA-VMamba-STRNet v2（Run8b 血统）：**拆除双曲几何后**的 CASA-VMamba 网络。

## 2026-10-20 之后的两件事，分清楚

**（a）拆除**：原来的 `geometry=hyperbolic|euclidean` 走 Lorentz 映射 + 半径分解 + detail/global
双分支特化 QKV，实测 `B1_HYP` 四库 ΔF1 均值 −0.00085 且更贵 ⇒ **已拆**。
现在 `geometry` 只接受 `"none"`/`"lattice"`，传旧值直接 `SystemExit`。

**（b）新增 Run9 HSAM**：`hsam=True` 打开 `model/layers/hsam.py` 的
**Poincaré 球双曲相似度注意力**（论文 JSTARS 2024 的 HSAM，**零参数**）。
它作用在编码器**最深两层**的输出上：exp 映射 → 双曲距离 → `HW×HW` 注意力矩阵 →
双向聚合 + 残差，得到 `F^H_1/F^H_2` 后**替换**原特征再进 TAR/DCR。
注意：这两件事的几何**不是同一个东西** —— (a) 是 Lorentz 上做"变化分数→半径分解"，
(b) 是 Poincaré 球上做"相似度注意力"。

现在：

| 项 | 拆除后 |
|---|---|
| `geometry` | **只接受 `"none"` / `"lattice"`**；传 `"hyperbolic"`/`"euclidean"` **直接 `SystemExit`** |
| HQKV 模块 | `caacp_hqkv_lattice.CAACPHQKVLattice`（无几何、无半径、无分数；格点压缩 SAT-QKV） |
| `hqkv_detail_window` / `hqkv_global_window` / `hqkv_r0` / `hqkv_a` | **已删除**（双窗口与半径参数失去意义） |
| `hgsl` | **`hqkv=1 且 hgsl=1` 直接报错**：几何拆掉后没有分数可喂，不再静默跳过 |
| 诊断口 | `hqkv_report()` / `hqkv_diag()` / `snapshot_gamma_grad()` / `contrib_norm()` 全部保留 |
| 注入点 | 不变：`delta` 加到 `TAR` 输出的 `feats[0]`（`inject_mode=dcr1`） |

## 保留的、与几何无关的资产（拆除时**故意没动**）

- VMamba-Tiny 主干移植与预训练加载（`load_pretrained` 212/222、`worst_diff=0.0`）；
- **反自锁**工程手段：每支 `rms_normalize` + 独立 γ 门 + 非零初值 + `contrib_norm` fail-fast；
- TAR / DCR 折叠、`switch_to_deploy()` 语义（HQKV 是推理期真实算子，不折叠）；
- `param_breakdown()` / `pretrain_load_stats()` 等诊断。

## fail-fast（Run8 教训的延续）

`train.py` 在 **step 1000** 检查 `contrib_norm`：若 < 1e-3 直接 `SystemExit`，
让「模块零贡献」在 20 分钟内暴露，而不是跑满 80K 才发现。
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from model.layers.caacp_hqkv_lattice import (
    GAMMA_INIT,
    HQKV_FORM,
    HQKV_SEED,
    LATTICE_WINDOW_DEFAULT,
    build_caacp_hqkv_lattice,
)
from model.layers.hsam import ATTN_STAGES_DEFAULT as HSAM_STAGES_DEFAULT
from model.layers.hsam import HSAM_FORM, LOGIT_NORM_DEFAULT as HSAM_LOGIT_NORM_DEFAULT
from model.layers.hsam import RADIUS_MODE_DEFAULT as HSAM_RADIUS_DEFAULT
from model.layers.hsam import build_hsam
from model.str_dcr import DCRDecoder
from model.str_tar import MultiScaleTAR
from model.vmamba_tiny import build_vmamba_tiny

ENCODER_DIMS = (96, 192, 384, 768)
STR_DIM = 192
# 拆除后只允许这两项；"hyperbolic"/"euclidean" 已被移除（见模块头）
GEOMETRIES = ("none", "lattice")
REMOVED_GEOMETRIES = ("hyperbolic", "euclidean", "hyp", "euc", "lorentz", "manifold")
INJECT_MODES = ("none", "dcr1", "dcr1_16")
HQKV_FORM_NAME = HQKV_FORM
FAILFAST_MIN_CONTRIB = 1e-3


class CASAVMambaSTRNetV2(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, vmamba_pretrained_path=None, hqkv=True, str_dim=STR_DIM,
                 geometry="lattice", hgsl=False, hgsl_detach_score=True,
                 inject_mode="dcr1",
                 hqkv_rank=32, hqkv_lite_dim=192,
                 hqkv_window=LATTICE_WINDOW_DEFAULT,
                 hqkv_attn_stages=(1, 2, 3),
                 rep_mode="full", fs_tar=False, vmamba_cfg=None,
                 hqkv_seed=HQKV_SEED, gamma_init=GAMMA_INIT,
                 hsam=False, hsam_stages=HSAM_STAGES_DEFAULT,
                 hsam_logit_norm=HSAM_LOGIT_NORM_DEFAULT,
                 hsam_radius=HSAM_RADIUS_DEFAULT,
                 hsam_feature_source="pre_norm"):
        super().__init__()
        g = str(geometry).lower()
        if g in REMOVED_GEOMETRIES:
            raise SystemExit(
                f"[HQKV-GEOMETRY-REMOVED] geometry='{geometry}' 已于 2026-10-20 拆除。\n"
                f"  原因：B1_HYP 四库 ΔF1 均值 −0.00085（1 平 3 负），且多付 0.304 M 参数 / "
                f"+0.46 G FLOPs / 约 +9% 时间。\n"
                f"  归档（字节可复核）："
                f"train_scripts/CASA-TViM/Run8b/_SUPERSEDED_hqkv_v2_2026-10-20/\n"
                f"  现在只接受 geometry in {GEOMETRIES}；要重开流形几何请先写新的预注册。\n"
                f"  注：Run9 的双曲模块是 **HSAM**（Poincaré 球、零参数、只作相似度），"
                f"由 `hsam=True` 打开，与本条无关。")
        if g not in GEOMETRIES:
            raise SystemExit(f"[HQKV-GEOMETRY] 不支持 {geometry!r}，只接受 {GEOMETRIES}")
        if hqkv and hgsl:
            raise SystemExit(
                "[HGSL-REMOVED] hqkv=1 且 hgsl=1 已不可用：几何拆除后 CAACP 不再产生变化分数，"
                "HGSL 没有输入口。\n"
                "  HGSL 的实现留在 models/model/hgsl_loss.py（未删），但需要先有分数来源；"
                "若要用它，必须另开一轮预注册说明分数从哪来。")
        if hsam and hgsl:
            raise SystemExit(
                "[HGSL-REMOVED] hsam=1 且 hgsl=1 已不可用：HSAM 不产生变化分数（它只重写特征），"
                "HGSL 没有输入口。")
        assert inject_mode in INJECT_MODES, inject_mode
        assert rep_mode in ("plain", "full")

        self.hqkv = bool(hqkv)
        self.geometry = g
        self.hgsl = bool(hgsl)
        self.hgsl_detach_score = bool(hgsl_detach_score)
        self.inject_mode = inject_mode
        self.rep_mode = rep_mode
        self.str_dim = int(str_dim)
        self.fs_tar = bool(fs_tar)
        self.hqkv_form = HQKV_FORM_NAME if self.hqkv else None
        self.gamma_init = float(gamma_init)
        self.hsam = bool(hsam)
        self.hsam_stages = tuple(int(s) for s in hsam_stages)
        self.hsam_form = HSAM_FORM if self.hsam else None
        self._failfast_done = False

        self.encoder = build_vmamba_tiny(pretrained_path=vmamba_pretrained_path,
                                         cfg=vmamba_cfg)
        self.head = nn.Conv2d(self.str_dim, 1, 1)
        self.tar = MultiScaleTAR(
            encoder_dims=ENCODER_DIMS, dim=self.str_dim,
            use_temporal_aux=(rep_mode == "full"),
            use_dcr_aux=(rep_mode == "full"), use_residual=True,
            fine_stage1=self.fs_tar,
        )
        self.decoder = DCRDecoder(dim=self.str_dim, use_aux=(rep_mode == "full"),
                                  use_residual=True)

        # HQKV **最后构造** + fork_rng（保证 RNG 消耗不影响主干初始化）
        self._diag = {}
        self._hsam_diag = {}
        if self.hqkv:
            with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):
                torch.manual_seed(int(hqkv_seed))
                self.hqkv_op = build_caacp_hqkv_lattice(
                    stage_dims=ENCODER_DIMS, rank=hqkv_rank,
                    window=hqkv_window,
                    attn_stages=hqkv_attn_stages, lite_dim=hqkv_lite_dim,
                    init_seed=int(hqkv_seed), gamma_init=gamma_init)
        else:
            self.hqkv_op = None
        # Run9：HSAM 零参数 ⇒ 不影响 RNG；放在 HQKV 之后再构造，保持既有 run 的 RNG 序列不变
        self.hsam_logit_norm = str(hsam_logit_norm)
        self.hsam_radius = str(hsam_radius)
        self.hsam_feature_source = str(hsam_feature_source)
        self.hsam_op = (build_hsam(stage_dims=ENCODER_DIMS, stages=self.hsam_stages,
                                   logit_norm=self.hsam_logit_norm,
                                   radius_mode=self.hsam_radius,
                                   feature_source=self.hsam_feature_source)
                        if self.hsam else None)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ 诊断
    def hqkv_report(self):
        if self.hqkv_op is None:
            return None
        rep = self.hqkv_op.param_report()
        rep.update({"geometry": self.geometry, "inject_mode": self.inject_mode,
                    "hgsl": self.hgsl, "hgsl_detach_score": self.hgsl_detach_score})
        return rep

    def hgsl_score_maps(self):
        """恒为 `{}`：几何拆除后没有变化分数（`hgsl=1` 在构造期已 fail-closed）。"""
        return {}

    def hqkv_diag(self):
        return {} if self.hqkv_op is None else dict(getattr(self.hqkv_op, "_diag", {}))

    def snapshot_gamma_grad(self):
        if self.hqkv_op is not None:
            self.hqkv_op.snapshot_gamma_grad()

    def contrib_norm(self):
        """当前 `contrib_norm`（已乘门）。fail-fast 用。"""
        d = self.hqkv_diag()
        return float(d.get("contrib_norm", 0.0))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Run9 HSAM
    def hsam_report(self):
        return None if self.hsam_op is None else self.hsam_op.param_report()

    def hsam_diag(self):
        return {} if self.hsam_op is None else self.hsam_op.diag()

    def set_hsam_collect(self, flag=True):
        """打开/关闭 HSAM 统计。默认关闭（每步统计会触发 device sync，白吃吞吐）。"""
        if self.hsam_op is not None:
            self.hsam_op.collect_diag = bool(flag)

    def pretrain_load_stats(self):
        return self.encoder.load_stats()

    def param_breakdown(self):
        enc = sum(p.numel() for p in self.encoder.parameters())
        tar = sum(p.numel() for p in self.tar.parameters())
        dcr = sum(p.numel() for p in self.decoder.parameters())
        head = sum(p.numel() for p in self.head.parameters())
        hq = (sum(p.numel() for p in self.hqkv_op.parameters())
              if self.hqkv_op is not None else 0)
        hs = (sum(p.numel() for p in self.hsam_op.parameters())
              if self.hsam_op is not None else 0)
        total = sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters())
        return {"backbone": enc, "hqkv": hq, "hsam": hs, "tar": tar, "dcr": dcr,
                "head": head,
                "total": total, "non_backbone": total - enc,
                "non_backbone_no_hqkv": total - enc - hq - hs}

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(self, pre, post, label=None):
        x2b = torch.cat([pre, post], dim=0)
        if self.hsam_op is not None and self.hsam_op.feature_source == "pre_norm":
            # Run9：HSAM 必须在 `outnorm`（LayerNorm2d）**之前**混合。
            # 原因（实测）：outnorm 让每个 token 的范数精确等于 √C ⇒ Poincaré 半径恒定
            # ⇒ 双曲距离退化成余弦距离的单调变换（秩相关 1.000000）。
            pre_taps = self.encoder.forward_pre_norm(x2b)
            del x2b
            a_pre, b_pre = [], []
            for t in pre_taps:
                a, b = t.chunk(2, dim=0)
                a_pre.append(a)
                b_pre.append(b)
            del pre_taps
            a_pre, b_pre = self.hsam_op(a_pre, b_pre)
            self._hsam_diag = self.hsam_op.diag()
            feats_a = [getattr(self.encoder, f"outnorm{i}")(a_pre[i])
                       for i in range(len(a_pre))]
            feats_b = [getattr(self.encoder, f"outnorm{i}")(b_pre[i])
                       for i in range(len(b_pre))]
            del a_pre, b_pre
        else:
            outs = self.encoder.forward_features(x2b)
            del x2b
            feats_a, feats_b = [], []
            for t in outs:
                a, b = t.chunk(2, dim=0)
                feats_a.append(a)
                feats_b.append(b)
            del outs
            if self.hsam_op is not None:      # post_norm 负对照（已证明退化，仅作对照）
                feats_a, feats_b = self.hsam_op(feats_a, feats_b)
                self._hsam_diag = self.hsam_op.diag()
            else:
                self._hsam_diag = {}

        if self.hqkv_op is not None:
            out, diag = self.hqkv_op(feats_a, feats_b)
            self._diag = diag
            if self.inject_mode in ("dcr1", "dcr1_16"):
                delta = out
                if self.inject_mode == "dcr1_16" and self.str_dim == delta.shape[1]:
                    feats_a[1] = feats_a[1] + F.interpolate(
                        delta, size=feats_a[1].shape[-2:], mode="bilinear",
                        align_corners=False)
            else:
                delta = None
        else:
            delta = None
            self._diag = {}

        feats = self.tar(feats_a, feats_b)
        if delta is not None:
            assert feats[0].shape[-2:] == delta.shape[-2:], \
                f"[INJECT] 尺度不匹配 {tuple(feats[0].shape[-2:])} vs {tuple(delta.shape[-2:])}"
            feats = list(feats)
            feats[0] = feats[0] + delta
        x = self.decoder(feats)
        logits = self.head(x)
        logits = F.interpolate(logits, size=pre.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear",
                               align_corners=False)
        return torch.sigmoid(logits)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        """折叠 TAR/DCR 的训练多分支；**HQKV 保留**（推理期真实算子）。

        理由：`train↔deploy` 折叠要求两图前向逐位一致，删掉 HQKV 会让 `feats[0]` 少一项。
        轻量主张只能来自它参数小，不能声称零推理代价。
        """
        self.tar.switch_to_deploy()
        self.decoder.switch_to_deploy()
        return self

# FILE: models/model\decoder.py
================================================================================
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from einops import rearrange

from model.utils import weight_init


def drop_path(x, drop_prob: float = 0., training: bool = False):
    """Drop paths (Stochastic Depth) per sample (when applied in main path of residual blocks).

    This is the same as the DropConnect impl I created for EfficientNet, etc networks, however,
    the original name is misleading as 'Drop Connect' is a different form of dropout in a separate paper...
    See discussion: https://github.com/tensorflow/tpu/issues/494#issuecomment-532968956 ... I've opted for
    changing the layer and argument names to 'drop path' rather than mix DropConnect as a layer name and use
    'survival rate' as the argument.

    """
    if drop_prob == 0. or not training:
        return x
    keep_prob = 1 - drop_prob
    shape = (x.shape[0],) + (1,) * (x.ndim - 1)  # work with diff dim tensors, not just 2D ConvNets
    random_tensor = keep_prob + torch.rand(shape, dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device)
    random_tensor.floor_()  # binarize
    output = x.div(keep_prob) * random_tensor
    return output

class DropPath(nn.Module):
    """Drop paths (Stochastic Depth) per sample  (when applied in main path of residual blocks).
    """
    def __init__(self, drop_prob=None):
        super(DropPath, self).__init__()
        self.drop_prob = drop_prob

    def forward(self, x):
        return drop_path(x, self.drop_prob, self.training)

class Mlp(nn.Module):
    """ MLP as used in Vision Transformer, MLP-Mixer and related networks
    """
    def __init__(self, in_features, hidden_features=None, out_features=None, act_layer=nn.GELU, drop=0.):
        super().__init__()
        out_features = out_features or in_features
        hidden_features = hidden_features or in_features
        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(in_features, hidden_features)
        self.act = act_layer()
        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(hidden_features, out_features)
        self.drop = nn.Dropout(drop)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.fc1(x)
        x = self.act(x)
        x = self.drop(x)
        x = self.fc2(x)
        x = self.drop(x)
        return x

class CrossAttention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dim1, dim2, num_heads=8, qkv_bias=False, attn_drop=0., proj_drop=0.):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        head_dim = dim1 // num_heads
        self.scale = head_dim ** -0.5

        self.q = nn.Linear(dim1, dim1, bias=qkv_bias)
        self.kv = nn.Linear(dim2, dim1 * 2, bias=qkv_bias)

        self.attn_drop = nn.Dropout(attn_drop)
        self.proj = nn.Linear(dim1, dim1)
        self.proj_drop = nn.Dropout(proj_drop)

    def forward(self, x, y):
        B1, N1, C1 = x.shape
        B2, N2, C2 = y.shape

        q = self.q(x).reshape(B1, N1, self.num_heads, C1 // self.num_heads).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        kv = self.kv(y).reshape(B2, N2, 2, self.num_heads, C1 // self.num_heads).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)

        k, v = kv[0], kv[1]

        attn = (q @ k.transpose(-2, -1)) * self.scale
        attn = attn.softmax(dim=-1)
        attn = self.attn_drop(attn)

        x = (attn @ v).transpose(1, 2).reshape(B1, N1, C1)

        x = self.proj(x)
        x = self.proj_drop(x)

        return x


class Block(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dim1, dim2, num_heads, mlp_ratio=4., qkv_bias=False, drop=0., attn_drop=0.,
                 drop_path=0., act_layer=nn.GELU, norm_layer=nn.LayerNorm):
        super().__init__()
        self.norm1 = norm_layer(dim1)        
        self.norm2 = norm_layer(dim2)
        self.attn = CrossAttention(dim1, dim2, num_heads=num_heads, qkv_bias=qkv_bias, attn_drop=attn_drop, proj_drop=drop)
        self.drop_path = DropPath(drop_path) if drop_path > 0. else nn.Identity()
        self.norm3 = norm_layer(dim1)
        mlp_hidden_dim = int(dim1 * mlp_ratio)
        self.mlp = Mlp(in_features=dim1, hidden_features=mlp_hidden_dim, act_layer=act_layer, drop=drop)

    def forward(self, x, y):
        x = x + self.drop_path(self.attn(self.norm1(x), self.norm2(y)))
        x = x + self.drop_path(self.mlp(self.norm3(x)))
        return x


class FeatureInjector(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dim1=384, dim2=[64, 128, 256], num_heads=8, mlp_ratio=4., qkv_bias=False, drop=0., attn_drop=0.,
                    drop_path=0., act_layer=nn.ReLU, norm_layer=nn.LayerNorm):
        super().__init__()

        self.c2_c5 = Block(dim1, dim2[0], num_heads, mlp_ratio, qkv_bias, drop, attn_drop, drop_path, act_layer, norm_layer)
        self.c3_c5 = Block(dim1, dim2[1], num_heads, mlp_ratio, qkv_bias, drop, attn_drop, drop_path, act_layer, norm_layer)
        self.c4_c5 = Block(dim1, dim2[2], num_heads, mlp_ratio, qkv_bias, drop, attn_drop, drop_path, act_layer, norm_layer)

        self.fuse = nn.Conv2d(dim1*3, dim1, 1, bias=False)

        weight_init(self)


    def base_forward(self, c2, c3, c4, c5):
        H, W = c5.shape[2:]

        c2 = rearrange(c2, 'b c h w -> b (h w) c')
        c3 = rearrange(c3, 'b c h w -> b (h w) c')
        c4 = rearrange(c4, 'b c h w -> b (h w) c')
        c5 = rearrange(c5, 'b c h w -> b (h w) c')

        _c2 = self.c2_c5(c5, c2)
        _c2 = rearrange(_c2, 'b (h w) c -> b c h w', h=H, w=W)

        _c3 = self.c3_c5(c5, c3)
        _c3 = rearrange(_c3, 'b (h w) c -> b c h w', h=H, w=W)

        _c4 = self.c4_c5(c5, c4)
        _c4 = rearrange(_c4, 'b (h w) c -> b c h w', h=H, w=W)

        _c5 = self.fuse(torch.cat([_c2, _c3, _c4], dim=1))

        return _c5

    def forward(self, fx, fy):
        _c5x = self.base_forward(fx[0], fx[1], fx[2], fx[3])
        _c5y = self.base_forward(fy[0], fy[1], fy[2], fy[3])

        return _c5x, _c5y


class Decoder(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_dim=[64, 128, 256, 384], decay=4, num_class=1):
        super().__init__()
        c2_channel, c3_channel, c4_channel, c5_channel = in_dim

        self.structure_enhance = FeatureInjector(dim1=c5_channel)

        self.up_c5 = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(c5_channel, c4_channel, 1, bias=False),
            nn.ConvTranspose2d(c4_channel, c4_channel, kernel_size=4, stride=2, padding=1)
        )

        self.up_c4 = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(c4_channel, c3_channel, 1, bias=False),
            nn.ConvTranspose2d(c3_channel, c3_channel, kernel_size=4, stride=2, padding=1)
        )

        self.up_c3 = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(c3_channel, c2_channel, 1, bias=False),
            nn.ConvTranspose2d(c2_channel, c2_channel, kernel_size=4, stride=2, padding=1)
        )

        self.classfier = nn.Sequential(
            nn.ConvTranspose2d(c2_channel, c2_channel, kernel_size=4, stride=2, padding=1),
            nn.Conv2d(c2_channel, num_class, 3, 1, padding=1, bias=False)
        )

        self.mlp = nn.ModuleList([
            nn.Sequential(
                nn.Conv2d(dim*3, dim//decay, 1, bias=False),
                nn.BatchNorm2d(dim//decay),
                nn.ReLU(),
                nn.Conv2d(dim//decay, dim//decay, 3, 1, padding=1, bias=False),
                nn.ReLU(),
                nn.Conv2d(dim//decay, dim//decay, 3, 1, padding=1, bias=False),
                nn.ReLU(),
                nn.Conv2d(dim//decay, dim, 3, 1, padding=1, bias=False)
            ) for dim in in_dim 
        ])

    def difference_modeling(self, x, y, block):
        f = torch.cat([x, y, torch.abs(x-y)], dim=1)
        f = block(f)

        return f

    def forward(self, fx, fy):
        c2x, c3x, c4x = fx[:-1]
        c2y, c3y, c4y = fy[:-1]

        c5x, c5y = self.structure_enhance(fx, fy)

        c2 = self.difference_modeling(c2x, c2y, self.mlp[0])
        c3 = self.difference_modeling(c3x, c3y, self.mlp[1])
        c4 = self.difference_modeling(c4x, c4y, self.mlp[2])
        c5 = self.difference_modeling(c5x, c5y, self.mlp[3])

        c4f = c4 + self.up_c5(c5)
        c3f = c3 + self.up_c4(c4f)
        c2f = c2 + self.up_c3(c3f)

        pred = self.classfier(c2f)
        pred_mask = torch.sigmoid(pred)

        return pred_mask

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\depth_pyramid_head.py
================================================================================
"""Run7 CSDP head：Cross-Depth Symmetric Difference + Sub-patch Expansion。

设计（CASA-CD_Run7_CSDP方案与预注册.md §4）：
  输入 fx = [B1_x, B2_x]、fy = [B1_y, B2_y]（DeiT-Tiny block1/2 输出过 final LN，
  各 B×256×192）。对每个深度 l∈{1,2} 构造严格时间交换对称的 pair descriptor：

      D_l = P_l([ |T_A^l − T_B^l| , (T_A^l + T_B^l)/2 ])     # 384 → 96
      Z   = D_1 + D_2                                        # B×256×96 → 96×16×16

  然后 Sub-patch Expansion（每级：Conv1×1 扩张 + BN + ReLU → PixelShuffle×2 →
  DWConv3×3 + BN + ReLU）：
      96×16×16 → 32×32×32 → 64×64×16 → 128×128×16 → 256×256×4 → 1×1 → sigmoid

  无独立 detail encoder、无 attention、无 residual、T1/T2 共享权重；
  head 只依赖 [|Δ|, mean] → pred(A,B) == pred(B,A)。

参数（设计值）：P1/P2 各 36,960（Linear(384→96, bias)）；expansion 各 stage 合计
16,912；**head 总计 90,832**。整体 = ViT2 1,087,104 + head 90,832 = 1,177,936。
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class DepthPyramidHead(nn.Module):
    """CSDP head（Semantic-Source Depth-Pyramid 变化重建）。"""

    def __init__(self, token_dim=192, proj_dim=96):
        super().__init__()
        self.p1 = nn.Linear(token_dim * 2, proj_dim, bias=True)
        self.p2 = nn.Linear(token_dim * 2, proj_dim, bias=True)

        # Sub-patch expansion：96×16×16 → 256×256
        self.exp1 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(96, 128, 1, bias=False), nn.BatchNorm2d(128), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.dw1 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(32, 32, 3, 1, padding=1, groups=32, bias=False),
                                 nn.BatchNorm2d(32), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.exp2 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 1, bias=False), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.dw2 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(16, 16, 3, 1, padding=1, groups=16, bias=False),
                                 nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.exp3 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(16, 64, 1, bias=False), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.dw3 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(16, 16, 3, 1, padding=1, groups=16, bias=False),
                                 nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.exp4 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(16, 16, 1, bias=False), nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.dw4 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(4, 4, 3, 1, padding=1, groups=4, bias=False),
                                 nn.BatchNorm2d(4), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.out = nn.Conv2d(4, 1, 1, bias=False)

    def _pair_descriptor(self, ta, tb, proj):
        d = torch.cat([torch.abs(ta - tb), (ta + tb) / 2.0], dim=-1)   # (B,256,384)
        return proj(d)                                                  # (B,256,96)

    def forward(self, fx, fy):
        b1x, b2x = fx
        b1y, b2y = fy
        z = self._pair_descriptor(b1x, b1y, self.p1) + self._pair_descriptor(b2x, b2y, self.p2)
        B, N, C = z.shape
        x = z.transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, C, 16, 16)

        x = self.dw1(F.pixel_shuffle(self.exp1(x), 2))      # 32×32×32
        x = self.dw2(F.pixel_shuffle(self.exp2(x), 2))      # 64×64×16
        x = self.dw3(F.pixel_shuffle(self.exp3(x), 2))      # 128×128×16
        x = self.dw4(F.pixel_shuffle(self.exp4(x), 2))      # 256×256×4
        return torch.sigmoid(self.out(x))

from functools import partial
import math
import logging
from typing import Sequence, Tuple, Union, Callable

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
import torch.utils.checkpoint
from torch.nn.init import trunc_normal_
from einops import rearrange

from model.layers import (
    Mlp,
    PatchEmbed,
    SwiGLUFFNFused,
    MemEffAttention,
    CASAAAttention,
    rank_normalize_per_image,
    NestedTensorBlock as Block,
)
from model.resnet import resnet18
from model.light_detail import LightDetail
from model.psd_detail import PSDDetail
