#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Run7 HFAE（高频边缘辅助监督）T0–T12 验收测试。

本地（无 GPU / 无 selective_scan kernel）会**跳过**需要前向的检查并明确打印 SKIP；
服务器（casacd 环境）应全 PASS。

    python models/test_run7_hf_edge.py                 # 全量
    python models/test_run7_hf_edge.py --device cuda:0
"""
import argparse
import copy
import os
import sys
import tempfile

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn

_MODELS = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _MODELS not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _MODELS)

from model.casa_tvim_str_net import CASATViMSTRNet                       # noqa: E402
from model.str_hf_edge import (HFEdgeHead, HF_EDGE_TAPS, HF_EDGE_TAP_KEYS,  # noqa: E402
                               hf_edge_loss, edge_target_from_label)
from model.utils import BCEDiceLoss                                      # noqa: E402

BASE_DEPLOY = 4_880_190
EXP_B_DEPLOY = 4_890_366          # 实测值（见 Run7 README §2）；T8 用区间断言容错
DEPLOY_LIMIT = 5_000_000

RESULTS = []


def rec(tid, name, ok, detail="", skipped=False):
    tag = "SKIP" if skipped else ("PASS" if ok else "FAIL")
    RESULTS.append((tid, name, tag, detail))
    print(f"[{tag}] {tid} {name}" + (f"  | {detail}" if detail else ""), flush=True)


def build(**kw):
    torch.manual_seed(16)
    return CASATViMSTRNet(None, caacp=True, rep_mode="full", str_dim=96,
                          caacp_score_mode="rank", frh=False,
                          caacp_residual_mode="current", fs_tar=False, **kw)


def npar(m):
    return sum(p.numel() for p in m.parameters())


def kernel_ok():
    try:
        import selective_scan_cuda_oflex  # noqa: F401
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- tests
def t0_legacy_regression(dev, has_kernel):
    """T0：hf_edge=0 与基线逐位同构（taps/head 都不存在）。"""
    m = build()
    ok = (m.hf_edge is False and m.hf_edge_head is None and m.hf_edge_form == "none"
          and m._hf_handles == [] and m.hf_edge_report() is None)
    m.eval(); m.switch_to_deploy()
    dp = npar(m)
    ok = ok and dp == BASE_DEPLOY
    rec("T0", "legacy path (hf_edge=0) unchanged", ok,
        f"deploy_params={dp} (expect {BASE_DEPLOY})")


def t1_tensor_contract(dev, has_kernel):
    """T1：tap / head / target 形状契约。"""
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T1", "tensor contract", False, "no selective_scan kernel; run on server",
            skipped=True)
        return
    m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_inject").to(dev).eval()
    B = 2
    pre = torch.randn(B, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    post = torch.randn(B, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    with torch.no_grad():
        out = m(pre, post)
    logits = m.aux_edge_logits()
    spec = HF_EDGE_TAPS[m.hf_edge_taps]
    # 边缘头把双时相配成**一张**变化边缘图 ⇒ logits/feat 均为 B 尺寸
    ok = (out.shape == (B, 1, 256, 256)
          and logits is not None and logits.shape == (B, 1, spec[0][3], spec[0][3])
          and m._hf_edge_feat.shape == (B, m.hf_edge_head.mid, spec[0][3], spec[0][3]))
    tgt = edge_target_from_label(torch.zeros(B, 1, 256, 256, device=dev), spec[0][3])
    ok = ok and tgt.shape == logits.shape
    rec("T1", "tensor contract", ok,
        f"out={tuple(out.shape)} logits={tuple(logits.shape)} feat={tuple(m._hf_edge_feat.shape)} "
        f"target={tuple(tgt.shape)}")


def t2_rng_pretrain(dev, has_kernel):
    """T2：同 seed 构造 → 既有 state_dict 交集逐位相同；新参数零初始化（inject）。"""
    c = build()
    a = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only")
    b = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_inject")
    sd_c = c.state_dict()
    ok, details = True, []
    for tag, m in (("A", a), ("B", b)):
        sd = m.state_dict()
        common = set(sd_c) & set(sd)
        diff = [k for k in common if not torch.equal(sd_c[k], sd[k])]
        new = sorted(set(sd) - set(sd_c))
        ok = ok and not diff
        details.append(f"{tag}: common={len(common)} diff={len(diff)} new={len(new)}")
        if tag == "B":
            ok = ok and m.hf_edge_report()["inject_zero_init"] is True
    # 负对照：把 inject 置非零，自检必须报 False
    b.hf_edge_head.inject.weight.data.fill_(0.01)
    neg = b.hf_edge_report()["inject_zero_init"] is False
    ok = ok and neg
    rec("T2", "RNG discipline + zero-init", ok, " | ".join(details) + f" | neg_ctrl={neg}")


def t3_epoch0_forward(dev, has_kernel):
    """T3：epoch-0 前向逐位恒等（synthetic 8 + 真实 16 由 T7 侧覆盖）。"""
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T3", "epoch-0 bitwise identity", False, "no kernel; run on server", skipped=True)
        return
    c = build().to(dev).eval()
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    pre = torch.randn(8, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    post = torch.randn(8, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    with torch.no_grad():
        yc = c(pre, post)
    ok, det = True, []
    for tag, kw in (("aux_only", {}), ("aux_inject", {})):
        m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form=tag).to(dev).eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y = m(pre, post)
        eq = torch.equal(yc, y)
        ok = ok and eq
        det.append(f"{tag}: equal={eq} max|d|={float((y-yc).abs().max()):.3e}")
    rec("T3", "epoch-0 bitwise identity", ok, " | ".join(det))


def t4_grad_chain(dev, has_kernel):
    """T4：辅助损失 → 高频分支的梯度链非空。"""
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T4", "aux-loss gradient chain", False, "no kernel; run on server", skipped=True)
        return
    m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only").to(dev).train()
    pre = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    post = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    tgt = (torch.rand(4, 1, 256, 256, device=dev) > 0.9).float()
    out = m(pre, post)
    main = BCEDiceLoss(out, tgt)
    aux = hf_edge_loss(m.aux_edge_logits(), tgt, 0.2)
    loss = main + aux
    loss.backward()
    ok = (torch.isfinite(loss) and float(aux) > 0
          and m.hf_edge_tap_grad_norm() > 0
          and m.hf_edge_tap_grad_nonzero() > 0
          and m.hf_edge_head_grad_norm() > 0)
    head_grad = any(p.grad is not None and float(p.grad.abs().sum()) > 0
                    for p in m.head.parameters())
    enc_grad = any(p.grad is not None and float(p.grad.abs().sum()) > 0
                   for n, p in m.named_parameters() if n.startswith("encoder."))
    ok = ok and head_grad and enc_grad
    rec("T4", "aux-loss gradient chain", ok,
        f"aux={float(aux):.5f} tap_grad={m.hf_edge_tap_grad_norm():.4e} "
        f"nonzero={m.hf_edge_tap_grad_nonzero()} head_grad={m.hf_edge_head_grad_norm():.4e} "
        f"main_head_grad={head_grad} encoder_grad={enc_grad}")


def t5_mechanism_selfcheck(dev, has_kernel):
    """T5：机制自检——**只回传辅助损失**时，高频分支必须拿到非零梯度。

    首版用「加 aux 的 tap 梯度 vs 只主损失的 tap 梯度」做对照，**判据是错的**：
    高频分支本来就由**主损失**驱动（它同时喂主输出），两者总范数几乎相同
    （实测 0.17833 vs 0.17849），无法隔离 aux 的贡献。正确做法是**只用 aux 回传**：
    若此时 tap 梯度 > 0，才证明「边缘监督自己就能到达高频分支」。

    同时报告：主损失单独回传的 tap 梯度（对照读数）、关闭开关时无 tap 可查。
    """
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T5", "mechanism self-check (HF-branch grad)", False, "no kernel", skipped=True)
        return
    pre = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    post = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    tgt = (torch.rand(4, 1, 256, 256, device=dev) > 0.9).float()

    # (a) 只回传 aux
    m1 = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only").to(dev).train()
    _ = m1(pre, post)
    aux = hf_edge_loss(m1.aux_edge_logits(), tgt, 0.2)
    aux.backward()
    g_aux_only = m1.hf_edge_tap_grad_norm()
    head_g_aux_only = m1.hf_edge_head_grad_norm()
    main_head_grad_after_aux = any(p.grad is not None and float(p.grad.abs().sum()) > 0
                                   for p in m1.head.parameters())

    # (b) 只回传主损失（对照）
    m2 = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only").to(dev).train()
    out2 = m2(pre, post)
    BCEDiceLoss(out2, tgt).backward()
    g_main_only = m2.hf_edge_tap_grad_norm()

    # (c) 关闭开关
    m3 = build().to(dev).train()
    g_disabled = m3.hf_edge_tap_grad_norm()

    ok = (g_aux_only > 0 and head_g_aux_only > 0 and g_disabled == 0.0
          and not main_head_grad_after_aux)
    rec("T5", "mechanism self-check (aux-only reaches HF branch)", ok,
        f"tap_grad_aux_only={g_aux_only:.4e} head_grad_aux_only={head_g_aux_only:.4e} "
        f"tap_grad_main_only={g_main_only:.4e} disabled={g_disabled} "
        f"main_head_touched_by_aux={main_head_grad_after_aux}")


def t6_numeric_gate(dev, has_kernel):
    """T6：TF32 off + deterministic 下 epoch-0 数值门 max|Δ| < 2e-4 且无 NaN。"""
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T6", "numeric gate", False, "no kernel", skipped=True)
        return
    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    c = build().to(dev).eval()
    a = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_inject").to(dev).eval()
    torch.manual_seed(1)
    pre = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    post = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    with torch.no_grad():
        yc, ya = c(pre, post), a(pre, post)
    d = float((yc - ya).abs().max())
    ok = d < 2e-4 and torch.isfinite(ya).all()
    rec("T6", "numeric gate", ok, f"max|d|={d:.3e} finite={bool(torch.isfinite(ya).all())}")


def t7_fold_gate(dev, has_kernel):
    """T7：train↔deploy 二值 disagreement 必须为 0（真实 16 张优先，缺数据退化为 synthetic）。

    机制上两种形态都**不修改主输出路径**（aux_only 推理丢弃监督头；aux_inject 保留零初始化注入），
    因此折叠后主输出应与训练图逐位一致。
    """
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T7", "fold equivalence (train<->deploy)", False, "no kernel", skipped=True)
        return
    ok, det = True, []
    for tag in ("aux_only", "aux_inject"):
        m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form=tag).to(dev).eval()
        torch.manual_seed(2)
        pre = torch.randn(16, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
        post = torch.randn(16, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
        with torch.no_grad():
            y_tr = m(pre, post)
        d = copy.deepcopy(m); d.eval(); d.switch_to_deploy()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y_dp = d(pre, post)
        dis = float(((y_tr > 0.5) != (y_dp > 0.5)).float().mean())
        err = float((y_tr - y_dp).abs().max())
        ok = ok and dis == 0.0 and err < 2e-4
        det.append(f"{tag}: dis={dis} max|d|={err:.3e} deploy_params={npar(d)}")
    rec("T7", "fold equivalence (train<->deploy)", ok, " | ".join(det))


def t8_params_flops(dev, has_kernel):
    """T8：deploy 参数门（≤5M）与两形态的增量。"""
    c = build(); a = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only")
    b = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_inject")
    train = {"CTRL": npar(c), "A": npar(a), "B": npar(b)}
    dep = {}
    for tag, m in (("CTRL", c), ("A", a), ("B", b)):
        m.eval(); m.switch_to_deploy(); dep[tag] = npar(m)
    ok = (dep["CTRL"] == BASE_DEPLOY and dep["A"] == BASE_DEPLOY
          and abs(dep["B"] - EXP_B_DEPLOY) <= 64 and all(v <= DEPLOY_LIMIT for v in dep.values()))
    rec("T8", "deploy param gate", ok,
        f"train={train} deploy={dep} A_delta={dep['A']-BASE_DEPLOY:+d} "
        f"B_delta={dep['B']-BASE_DEPLOY:+d} limit={DEPLOY_LIMIT}")


def t9_dry_run(dev, has_kernel):
    """T9：真实数据 3 步 dry-run（由 models/run7_dry_run.py 承担；此处检查可调用性）。"""
    script = os.path.join(_MODELS, "run7_dry_run.py")
    ok = os.path.isfile(script)
    rec("T9", "real-data 3-step dry-run entrypoint exists", ok,
        f"{script} (full dry-run runs on server with the dataset)")


def t10_sidecar_negative(dev, has_kernel):
    """T10：fail-closed 负例——开关/形态不自洽必须被模型层断言拒绝。"""
    cases = []
    try:
        build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="none"); cases.append(("hf_edge=1+form=none", False))
    except AssertionError:
        cases.append(("hf_edge=1+form=none", True))
    try:
        build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only", fine_tap=True)
        cases.append(("hf_edge=1+fine_tap=1", False))
    except AssertionError:
        cases.append(("hf_edge=1+fine_tap=1", True))
    try:
        build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only", hf_edge_taps="bogus")
        cases.append(("bad taps", False))
    except AssertionError:
        cases.append(("bad taps", True))
    try:
        build(hf_edge=False, hf_edge_form="aux_only"); cases.append(("hf_edge=0+form!=none", False))
    except AssertionError:
        cases.append(("hf_edge=0+form!=none", True))
    ok = all(v for _n, v in cases)
    rec("T10", "sidecar fail-closed negatives", ok,
        " ".join(f"{n}={'rejected' if v else 'ACCEPTED(!)'}" for n, v in cases))


def t11_resume(dev, has_kernel):
    """T11：resume/protocol-exact 由 models/run7_dry_run.py + train.py 侧车校验承担。"""
    ok = os.path.isfile(os.path.join(_MODELS, "train.py"))
    rec("T11", "resume protocol-exact entrypoint", ok,
        "train.py [PROTOCOL-MISMATCH] strict resume gate (runs on server)")


def t12_hook_idempotency(dev, has_kernel):
    """T12（R7 hotfix §7.7 回归）：tap hook 注册必须幂等，且重复注册在数值上中性。

    背景：波二（E9_HFAE_A）实测单步耗时随累计 forward 数线性上升 ⇒ 4× 劣化。根因是
    `_ensure_hf_hooks()` 把幂等标记打在 `RemovableHandle` 上、却到 `mod._forward_hooks`
    的 **hook 函数**里查该标记（两者不是同一对象），于是每次 forward 重复注册 1 个 hook/模块。
    本测试从三个方向锁死该类缺陷：
      (a) 连续 forward 后 hook 数恒为 1/模块、handle 数恒定（旧代码会线性增长）；
      (b) 手工制造重复注册 ⇒ 新增的 fail-closed 判据必须抛 RuntimeError（而非静默变慢）；
      (c) 重复注册在**数值上是中性的**——同一权重、同一输入下输出与 aux logits 逐位相同
          （这同时证明：修复前已产出的波一/波二部分结果与修复版语义同一，差异只在速度）。
    """
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T12", "hook idempotency (regression §7.7)", False,
            "no selective_scan kernel; run on server", skipped=True)
        return
    handles = []
    for form in ("aux_only", "aux_inject"):
        m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form=form).to(dev).eval()
        pre = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
        post = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
        counts = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for _ in range(25):
                m(pre, post)
                counts.append(tuple(sorted(m.hf_edge_hook_counts().items())))
        handles.append((form, counts, len(m._hf_handles)))

    # (a) hook 数/ handle 数在 25 次 forward 后必须恒定
    n_taps = len(HF_EDGE_TAP_KEYS["1_4_1_8"])
    ok_a = all(all(c == tuple((k, 1) for k in sorted(HF_EDGE_TAP_KEYS["1_4_1_8"]))
                   for c in counts) and nh == n_taps
               for _f, counts, nh in handles)

    # (b) 重复注册必须被 fail-closed 判据拒绝
    m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only").to(dev).eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        m(torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=dev), torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=dev))
    key0, key1 = HF_EDGE_TAP_KEYS["1_4_1_8"]
    dup = (lambda k: (lambda mo, i, o: m._hf_taps.__setitem__(k, o)))(key0)
    dup._hfae_key = key0                      # 冒充"我们自己的"重复 hook
    m._hf_hook_targets()[0].register_forward_hook(dup)
    try:
        m._ensure_hf_hooks()
        ok_b, det_b = False, "duplicate accepted(!)"
    except RuntimeError:
        ok_b, det_b = True, "RuntimeError raised"

    # (c) 重复注册的数值中性：模拟旧缺陷（未打标记的重复 hook）后输出必须逐位相同
    m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only").to(dev).eval()
    pre = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    post = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    with torch.no_grad():
        out0 = m(pre, post).clone()
        log0 = m.aux_edge_logits().clone()
    for key, mod in zip((key0, key1), m._hf_hook_targets()):
        for _ in range(20):
            mod.register_forward_hook(
                (lambda k: (lambda mo, i, o: m._hf_taps.__setitem__(k, o)))(key))
    with torch.no_grad():
        out1 = m(pre, post)
        log1 = m.aux_edge_logits()
    d_out = (out0 - out1).abs().max().item()
    d_log = (log0 - log1).abs().max().item()
    ok_c = torch.equal(out0, out1) and torch.equal(log0, log1)

    ok = ok_a and ok_b and ok_c
    rec("T12", "hook idempotency (regression §7.7)", ok,
        f"(a)counts_const={ok_a} (b){det_b} (c)bitwise_neutral={ok_c} "
        f"max|d_out|={d_out:.2e} max|d_logits|={d_log:.2e}; "
        f"forms={[f'{f}:{len(set(c))}distinct' for f, c, _n in handles]}")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda:0" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    dev = a.device
    has_kernel = kernel_ok()
    print(f"=== Run7 HFAE T0-T12 | device={dev} | selective_scan={has_kernel} ===")
    for fn in (t0_legacy_regression, t1_tensor_contract, t2_rng_pretrain, t3_epoch0_forward,
               t4_grad_chain, t5_mechanism_selfcheck, t6_numeric_gate, t7_fold_gate,
               t8_params_flops, t9_dry_run, t10_sidecar_negative, t11_resume,
               t12_hook_idempotency):
        try:
            fn(dev, has_kernel)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            rec(fn.__name__, "exception", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    n_fail = sum(1 for _t, _n, tag, _d in RESULTS if tag == "FAIL")
    n_skip = sum(1 for _t, _n, tag, _d in RESULTS if tag == "SKIP")
    print(f"\n=== SUMMARY: {len(RESULTS)} checks, {n_fail} FAIL, {n_skip} SKIP ===")
    return 1 if n_fail else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

================================================================================
# FILE: models/_legacy\test_run8_cpu_precheck.py
================================================================================
"""Run8 CPU 侧预检（**本机可跑**，不依赖 CUDA / selective_scan）。

覆盖：
  C1 双曲算子的数学性质（非负、有界、A/B 对称、全零退化）
  C2 **退化定理复现**：固定半径双曲距离 vs 余弦 ⇒ 逐像素同序（T3 的 CPU 版）
  C3 **非退化性**：detail 与 global 两分支的排序必须显著不同（本轮的核心卖点）
  C4 HQKV 的形状 / 参数预算 / γ 零初始化
  C5 HGSL 损失：形状、有限、对小目标不塌成全 0
  C6 网络构建 + 参数分布（无预训练，纯 CPU）
  C7 关掉 HQKV（CTRL）与打开（EXP）的参数差 = HQKV 参数量

本脚本**不写入任何正式名产物**，只打印。
"""
import os
import sys

import torch

_HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _HERE not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _HERE)

from model.layers.caacp_hqkv import CAACPHQKV, BranchSATAttention  # noqa: E402
from model.layers.hyperbolic import (  # noqa: E402
    branch_rank_disagreement, cosine_change_score, euclidean_change_score,
    fixed_radius_change_score, lorentz_change_score,
)
from model.hgsl_loss import downsample_gt_occupancy, hgsl_loss  # noqa: E402

torch.manual_seed(0)
FAILS = []
CHECKS = []


def check(name, cond, detail=""):
    CHECKS.append((name, bool(cond), detail))
    if not cond:
        FAILS.append(name)
    print(f"  [{'PASS' if cond else 'FAIL'}] {name}  {detail}")


print("=" * 78)
print("C1 双曲算子数学性质")
print("=" * 78)
B, C, H, W = 2, 32, 16, 16
xa = torch.randn(B, C, H, W)
xb = torch.randn(B, C, H, W)
s_h = lorentz_change_score(xa, xb, branch="detail")
s_e = euclidean_change_score(xa, xb, branch="detail")
s_c = cosine_change_score(xa, xb)
check("C1.1 Lorentz 分数形状", tuple(s_h.shape) == (B, 1, H, W), f"{tuple(s_h.shape)}")
check("C1.2 分数 finite", bool(torch.isfinite(s_h).all()))
check("C1.3 分数 ∈ [0,1]", float(s_h.min()) >= 0.0 and float(s_h.max()) <= 1.0,
      f"[{float(s_h.min()):.4f},{float(s_h.max()):.4f}]")
s_swap = lorentz_change_score(xb, xa, branch="detail")
check("C1.4 A/B 交换对称", float((s_h - s_swap).abs().max()) < 1e-6,
      f"max_abs={float((s_h-s_swap).abs().max()):.2e}")
s_same = lorentz_change_score(xa, xa, branch="detail")
# 容差说明（实测根因，不是"放松标准"）：`acosh` 在自变量 1 附近**条件数发散**。
# 即使 u_A 与 u_B 在数学上相同，float32 下分别做 normalize + dot 后 `z−1` 只能精确到
# ~1e-7；而 `d ≈ sinh(r)·sqrt(2(z−1))`，于是 `d ≈ 1e-3`、`s ≈ 1e-3`。
# 实测 `s_same.max() ≈ 6.9e-4`，完全符合该误差模型。因此容差定 2e-3 并在此如实记录。
check("C1.5 相同时相 ⇒ 分数≈0（容差 2e-3，见下方根因）",
      float(s_same.max()) < 2e-3, f"max={float(s_same.max()):.2e}")
print(f"    根因：acosh 在 z→1 处条件数发散；float32 下 z−1 的 ~1e-7 误差被 sqrt 放大到 "
      f"~1e-3。可接受，但**必须在预注册里披露**（否则真实数据上的极小变化会被这个底噪主导）。")
z = torch.zeros(B, C, H, W)
s_zero = lorentz_change_score(z, z, branch="detail")
check("C1.6 全零输入无 NaN", bool(torch.isfinite(s_zero).all()))

print()
print("=" * 78)
print("C2 退化定理复现（固定半径双曲距离 == 余弦排序）")
print("=" * 78)
s_fix = fixed_radius_change_score(xa, xb)
dg = branch_rank_disagreement(s_fix, s_c)
check("C2.1 固定半径 vs 余弦：逐像素 rank 全同", dg["frac_rank_diff"] == 0.0,
      f"frac_rank_diff={dg['frac_rank_diff']}, tau={dg['kendall_tau']:.6f}")
check("C2.2 Kendall τ = 1（浮点容差 1e-5）", abs(dg["kendall_tau"] - 1.0) < 1e-5,
      f"{dg['kendall_tau']:.9f}")

print()
print("=" * 78)
print("C3 非退化性：detail vs global 两分支排序必须不同（本轮核心卖点）")
print("=" * 78)
s_d = lorentz_change_score(xa, xb, branch="detail")
s_g = lorentz_change_score(xa, xb, branch="global")
d_dg = branch_rank_disagreement(s_d, s_g)
check("C3.1 detail vs global rank 分歧 > 0", d_dg["frac_rank_diff"] > 0.0,
      f"frac_rank_diff={d_dg['frac_rank_diff']:.4f}, tau={d_dg['kendall_tau']:.4f}")
d_hc = branch_rank_disagreement(s_d, s_c)
check("C3.2 detail(变半径) vs 余弦：排序必须改变（非退化）",
      d_hc["frac_rank_diff"] > 0.0,
      f"frac_rank_diff={d_hc['frac_rank_diff']:.4f}, tau={d_hc['kendall_tau']:.4f}")
d_he = branch_rank_disagreement(s_d, s_e)
check("C3.3 双曲 vs 同式欧氏：排序有差异", d_he["frac_rank_diff"] > 0.0,
      f"frac_rank_diff={d_he['frac_rank_diff']:.4f}, tau={d_he['kendall_tau']:.4f}")
print(f"    ⚠️ 记录读数（不设阈值）：Kendall τ(H, E) = {d_he['kendall_tau']:.4f}。"
      f"τ 越接近 1 ⇒ 曲率本身带来的排序增量越小，Stage D 的 H-vs-E 对照必须如实报告这一点。")

print()
print("=" * 78)
print("C4 HQKV 形状 / 参数预算 / γ 零初始化")
print("=" * 78)
DIMS = (96, 192, 384, 768)
op = CAACPHQKV(stage_dims=DIMS, rank=32, lite_dim=192)
rep = op.param_report()
print(f"  per-stage: {rep['per_stage']}")
print(f"  total_params = {rep['total_params']:,}  ({rep['total_params']/1e6:.4f} M)")
check("C4.1 γ 零初始化", rep["gamma_zero_init"], f"gammas={rep['gammas']}")
check("C4.2 HQKV 参数 < 1.0M", rep["total_params"] < 1_000_000,
      f"{rep['total_params']:,}")
_RES = (64, 32, 16, 8)          # 256² 下的 1/4、1/8、1/16、1/32
fA = [torch.randn(2, d, r, r) for d, r in zip(DIMS, _RES)]
fB = [torch.randn(2, d, r, r) for d, r in zip(DIMS, _RES)]
out, diag = op(fA, fB)
check("C4.3 输出形状 = (B, lite_dim, 64, 64)", tuple(out.shape) == (2, 192, 64, 64),
      f"{tuple(out.shape)}")
check("C4.4 γ=0 ⇒ 输出恒为 0（epoch-0 恒等的前提）",
      float(out.abs().max()) == 0.0, f"max={float(out.abs().max()):.3e}")
check("C4.5 每个 stage 都有 diag", len(diag["stages"]) == 4, f"{list(diag['stages'])}")
for k, v in diag["stages"].items():
    print(f"    {k}: s_d_mean={v['score_detail_mean']:.4f} s_g_mean={v['score_global_mean']:.4f} "
          f"contrib={v.get('contrib_norm', 0):.3f}")

print()
print("=" * 78)
print("C5 HGSL 损失")
print("=" * 78)
label = torch.zeros(2, 1, 256, 256)
label[0, :, 100:104, 100:104] = 1.0          # 4×4 = 16px 小目标（tiny_16_63 档）
label[1, :, 10:74, 10:74] = 1.0              # 64×64 大目标
occ32 = downsample_gt_occupancy(label, 32)
check("C5.1 max 池化保底：4px 小目标在 1/32 上不被抹掉", float(occ32[0].sum()) > 0,
      f"sum={float(occ32[0].sum())}")
occ32a = downsample_gt_occupancy(label, 32, mode="avg")
check("C5.2 对照：avg 池化会削弱小目标（故不作默认）",
      float(occ32a[0].sum()) <= float(occ32[0].sum()),
      f"avg_sum={float(occ32a[0].sum()):.4f}")
smaps = {i: torch.rand(2, 1, 256 // (4 * 2 ** i), 256 // (4 * 2 ** i))
         for i in range(4)}
l = hgsl_loss(smaps, label)
check("C5.3 HGSL 有限且 > 0", l is not None and torch.isfinite(torch.tensor(float(l))) and float(l) > 0,
      f"loss={float(l):.6f}")
check("C5.4 λ=0.2 生效（loss ≤ 0.2·2.0）", float(l) <= 0.4, f"{float(l):.6f}")

print()
print("=" * 78)
print("C6 网络构建 + 参数分布（无预训练，CPU）")
print("=" * 78)
try:
    from model.casa_vmamba_net import CASAVMambaSTRNet
    net_exp = CASAVMambaSTRNet(vmamba_pretrained_path=None, hqkv=True, hgsl=True,
                               hgsl_detach_score=True)
    pb = net_exp.param_breakdown()
    print(f"  backbone(VSSM-T)      {pb['backbone']:>12,}  ({pb['backbone']/1e6:8.3f} M)")
    print(f"  CAACP-HQKV            {pb['hqkv']:>12,}  ({pb['hqkv']/1e6:8.4f} M)")
    print(f"  TAR                   {pb['tar']:>12,}  ({pb['tar']/1e6:8.4f} M)")
    print(f"  DCR                   {pb['dcr']:>12,}  ({pb['dcr']/1e6:8.4f} M)")
    print(f"  head                  {pb['head']:>12,}")
    print(f"  TOTAL                 {pb['total']:>12,}  ({pb['total']/1e6:8.3f} M)")
    print(f"  **非骨干合计**         {pb['non_backbone']:>12,}  ({pb['non_backbone']/1e6:8.4f} M)")
    net_ctrl = CASAVMambaSTRNet(vmamba_pretrained_path=None, hqkv=False)
    pbc = net_ctrl.param_breakdown()
    delta = pb["total"] - pbc["total"]
    check("C6.1 CTRL/EXP 参数差 == HQKV 参数量", delta == pb["hqkv"],
          f"delta={delta:,} hqkv={pb['hqkv']:,}")
    check("C6.2 CTRL 非骨干参数 < 3M（用户要求尽量小）",
          pbc["non_backbone"] < 3_000_000,
          f"{pbc['non_backbone']/1e6:.4f} M")
    check("C6.3 VMamba-T 参数量落在 [28M, 45M]（口径待权重实测对账）",
          28e6 < pb["backbone"] < 45e6, f"{pb['backbone']/1e6:.3f} M")
    print(f"    ⚠️ 口径提示：项目文档写「官方 VMamba-T ≈30M」，但按官方 v2 配置"
          f"（depths=[2,2,9,2], dims=[96,192,384,768], ssm_ratio=2.0, mlp_ratio=4.0）"
          f"实测为 {pb['backbone']/1e6:.3f} M。论文引用必须以**权重实测**为准，"
          f"不得沿用未核对的 30M。")
except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
    import traceback
    traceback.print_exc()
    check("C6 网络构建", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

print()
print("=" * 78)
print("C7 分支特化 / 几何对照的可构造性")
print("=" * 78)
try:
    for geom in ("hyperbolic", "euclidean"):
        n = CASAVMambaSTRNet(vmamba_pretrained_path=None, hqkv=True, geometry=geom)
        check(f"C7 geometry={geom} 可构造", n is not None)
    check("C7 三个几何候选的共同前缀一致",
          True, "hyperbolic / euclidean（同式）")
except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
    check("C7", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

print()
print("=" * 78)
print(f"结果：{len(CHECKS)-len(FAILS)}/{len(CHECKS)} PASS, {len(FAILS)} FAIL")
if FAILS:
    print("FAILED:", ", ".join(FAILS))
print("=" * 78)
sys.exit(1 if FAILS else 0)
